# Terra Firma — Revival Tracker

Working list for finishing the mod. Update as things land.

**Resolved history lives in TODO-archive.md** (split 2026-08-06): build records, verify-session logs, superseded specs, decomp ground truth, harness rounds. RULED markers there still count as provenance - grep both files.

**Tags:** `[bug]` broken behavior · `[polish]` text/UX cleanup · `[design]` needs a creative/balance decision · `[verify]` needs in-game confirmation · `[stretch]` stretch goal

**How we work (2026-07-21):** each mutation gets **one audit pass** — knock out its small fixes, settle its design questions, restyle its description text (base-game voice, see style notes under Gorgon's Gaze), then mark it ready to verify. In-game verification happens in batches, not per-fix. ~~Fissure is vetoed until every other mutation's audit is complete.~~ **Veto condition met; lifted 2026-07-22 — Fissure is the active workstream.**

**Provenance discipline (2026-07-25, after the "gear-warfare doesn't cook" creep — a Claude
proposal hardened into a cited "rule" through nothing but repetition):** every design-shaping
statement in the trackers carries a status tag — **RULED** (date + the user's words) · **LEAN**
(user's, explicitly not a ruling) · **CLAUDE PROPOSAL, UNRULED**. Citing a rule as precedent
requires finding its RULED marker; no marker = it's a proposal and gets cited as one. Scope:
design claims only — delegated code/plumbing decisions need no ratification ceremony.

**0.1.1 UPDATE STAGED 2026-08-07 (user patch notes written; distribution = STEAM WORKSHOP, WorkshopId 3778701376 "Terra Firma Beta" — the `Mods\TerraFirmaBeta` folder is the upload staging copy, friends are subscribers):** dev tree synced into the staging folder (90 files hash-verified) with the beta's identity files preserved (`manifest.json` id `Cleo_TerraFirmaBeta`, `workshop.json`, `preview.png`); `publish\TerraFirma` mirrored + `CoQ_TerraFirma_beta_2026-08-07.zip` built as the record/manual-download fallback. **PUSHED TO WORKSHOP 2026-08-07 (user: "should be pushed now, yay") — 0.1.1 IS LIVE; subscribers auto-update on next launch.** Beta ships WITH the harness/instrumentation by standing decision (TF-VERIFY stays on for the beta); the strip obligation still gates the eventual full release.

- [ ] `[bug?]` **LIVE INCIDENT 2026-07-24 (verify-boot 15:42, character "Karuukat" Mutated Human Stonecarver + Calcify + Boar Vessel): per-tick NRE storm, cause UNATTRIBUTED.** Signature: `ERROR Registry.Add Index: -1` twice DURING Joppa zone build (base objects: Wooden Water Wheel/LiquidCovered, WatervineFarmerJoppa/Body), then `RunSegment (final): NullReferenceException at GameObject.TurnTick [0x0003e]` EVERY tick thereafter (27k+ in 20 min, log 12+ MB). Decomp (2.0.211.50): TurnTick walks the registered-handler array — a null Handler entry in a TurnTick registry, i.e. event-registry corruption from build time. Evidence FOR vanilla: zero mod frames in any stack; same class as verification session 1's "vanilla-only event-pool overflow in pit generation" scare; the failing registrations are pure base objects. Evidence AGAINST: first-ever occurrence is the first boot after the instrumentation deploy (prev boot 01:28 same day, same game version, same mod enabled, played clean with 191 TF-VERIFY lines) — though the four instrumented files (Lithovore eat channels, StoneMill menus, TimedForm, Stoneshaper cast) have no per-tick paths and none of their content exists in a fresh Joppa. Deployed folder hash-verified identical to repo; compile clean (37 files). Key fact: the embark auto-save COMPLETED before the first NRE — the on-disk save likely predates the corruption. **RESOLVED-AS-VANILLA 2026-07-24 (same day): relaunch + reload of the same save came back CLEAN** — zero Registry.Add errors, zero NREs, save loaded in 970ms, same mod build byte-for-byte. Verdict: a nondeterministic build-time event-registry race in the base game (second sighting of the class, after verification session 1's pit-generation scare). The instrumentation deploy is cleared — same code produced the corruption-free boot. Downgraded to a WATCH ITEM: if a third sighting lands, capture the log and consider reporting upstream to Freehold. The one MODERROR each boot is the disabled workshop mod "Bit Helper"'s broken manifest JSON — scan-time noise, not ours, ignorable.
- [x] `[bug]` **SAVE-COMPAT SHIM for the beta update — AUDITED + BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-08-07 (user: "the last thing i'm worried about ... is save compatibility"; compiles green, hash-verified).** Full category audit vs the friends' build (TerraFirmaBeta snapshot): NO classes removed (63/63 present), NO blueprints removed (187 all present), and the engine quarantines part-load failures in length-prefixed blocks (decomp `IPart.Load`/`SkipBlock`). Exactly TWO serialized-shape changes existed, both now shimmed with a **sentinel header** (`Helpers.SERIAL_SENTINEL` + format-version int — SHIPPING machinery, NOT part of the VERIFY_WATCH strip) plus a `ReadError` recovery that re-parses legacy blocks against an exact end-of-block alignment gate: **(1) `Part_Etchable`** (rides EVERY wall via the merge — unshimmed, every wall of an old save would log an engine exception on zone thaw, and the positional misread could overrun into neighboring parts): v2 local blocks (2026-08-05..07, bare-count format) recover IN PLACE with inscriptions intact; v1 friends-beta blocks (bare string) reset to blank quietly. **(2) `Cleo_TerraFirma_EarthenBarrage`** (the `WasOnWorldMap` field changed the positional layout, and EB's all-primitive tail means the misread would NOT throw — silent stream overrun, the audit's nastiest find): pre-sentinel LOCAL blocks recover perfectly (charges + level intact); friends-beta blocks reset clean — **the one real casualty: a beta tester's EB mutation resets (level included) on first load. CAUTION NOTE FOR THE UPDATE POST: EB characters should note their EB level before updating (or finish the run); everything else migrates silently — etched inscriptions from the FRIENDS' build are also lost (walls re-etch fine).** Benign-stale on old saves, no action: existing boar keeps the garbled name (in-game [r]ename fixes it), existing statues keep "intricately", old boars keep pottery anatomy; blueprint-tag changes (salt bleed, popup eligibility) apply instantly. `[verify]` tfserial round-trips the new sentinel format; cross-build check = load a pre-update save, grep the watcher for `[SERIAL]` recovery lines and ZERO engine deserialization exceptions.

## Gorgon's Gaze  ← audit in progress, furthest along

*(resolved -> TODO-archive.md: Done / The big one)*

### Open bugs / verifications
- [x] `[bug]` **MAJOR: gazing a wall = endless-pass LOCKOUT — DIAGNOSED + FIXED + DEPLOYED 2026-08-07 (user report from live play, "gorgon's gaze a holographic shale wall ... endless cycle of passing, not a hard freeze"; compiles green, hash-verified, in tfbatch).** Root cause chain (decomp-verified): the player-path picker's `GetCombatTarget(ParentObject, true)` rides the engine default **`AllowInanimate=true`**, so ANY plain wall or furniture is a legal gaze victim — the holographic part was incidental (its invulnerability never even engages). A brainless Target then NREs `TickGaze`'s `Target.Brain.Attacked` on EVERY BeginTakeActionEvent; the engine aborts the player's action each segment, and the gaze can never break because the break rides UseEnergyEvent — an aborted action never spends energy. Same signature class as the 07-24 TurnTick NRE storm: game live, player locked. **The user's animated-wall theory checked and inverted: an ANIMATED wall is a full creature (Brain + donor stat block, and AnimateEvent strips HologramInvulnerability) — it duels and resolves fine; only PLAIN inanimates lock.** Fix, two layers: (1) command gate — brainless victims refused before the energy/cooldown spend, both player path (ShowFail "You cannot transfix {it}." — DRAFT) and AI path (silent invalid); every true creature in Qud carries a Brain, mindless included, so nothing legit is excluded; (2) TickGaze belt — a brainless Target releases silently instead of NRE-ing, which also **SELF-HEALS any save made inside the lockout** (first tick after load releases the gaze). Harness: `feedback.gazewall` drives BeginGaze→TickGaze against a spawned HolographicShaleWall and asserts release-not-throw. `[verify]` via **`tfgazewall`** (user test design, one-wish staging: plain + animated holo wall side by side) — **LIVE RUN 2026-08-07 morning boot, log-confirmed: the ANIMATED wall duel ran end-to-end** (saves rolled with 2 interleaved successes, DC escalated 12→21 per the +1/fail law, band FX tracked net 0→7, 10th cumulative fail → PETRIFIED, statue + entombed possessions, zero exceptions in the log) **and `feedback.gazewall` PASS same boot** (brainless release belt fired, no NRE). **PLAIN wall refusal CONFIRMED same boot (user: "the holoshale wall refused me so that's good") — the gaze-wall fix is FULLY GREEN:** gate ✓ duel ✓ belt ✓. Residual (only if a locked save ever surfaces): the self-heal ride is code-identical to the harness belt, expected to just work.
- [ ] `[bug?]` `[verify]` **Dead-gorgon trap — DEFERRED FROM THE VERIFY PASS (user ruling 2026-07-24: "no NPCs have gorgon's gaze, so we can flag those for not needing verification right now"):** the fix (effect self-recedes when `Initiator` invalid, own EndTurnEvent) is BUILT and instrumented (`GAZE DEAD-GORGON RELEASE` line); it just cannot fire until an NPC gorgon exists to kill mid-channel. Verify lands with the creatures/quarriers workstream (same box as the parked LOS-break dial). Original diagnosis kept: if the caster dies mid-gaze, `TickGaze` stops firing and the `Petrifying` effect doesn't count down while active.
- [ ] `[verify]` **Stunned/confused/asleep caster — DEFERRED FROM THE VERIFY PASS (same 2026-07-24 ruling, same gate):** needs an NPC gorgon to stun. Sunder Mind explicitly breaks its channel on such effects; Gorgon's has no equivalent check — test when NPC gorgons exist; add an explicit check if a stunned gorgon keeps gazing.
- [ ] `[verify]` Confirm Willpower mod lands in the save DC (enable the show-save-rolls debug option, gaze once, read the log).

## Earthen Barrage

*(resolved -> TODO-archive.md: Small fixes)*

- [x] **Feedback-thread item CHECKED, ALREADY IMPLEMENTED (2026-08-07, user: "earthen barrage should visually become larger stones as you level ... ignore me if it already does"):** it does — pebble/stone/boulder at Lv1/5/10 across name (`GetStoneName`), sprite (`GetStoneCosmeticData`: sw_smallstone → sw_stone_large → sw_mediumboulder), AND throw/impact sfx weight. The reporter has likely only seen sub-Lv5 pebbles. Known non-issue: a held stone doesn't re-dress at the threshold moment, but every throw/re-arm sculpts fresh and held stones ride a short lease — stale window is a few turns, no code warranted.

### Design calls
- [ ] `[design]` **Daze rider CUT — RULED 2026-08-06 (beta rebalance pass, item 3; user: "cutting the daze rider from earthen barrage") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** Constants, `GetThrowDazeChanceIn100`, the `MakeStone` part attachment, the level-text line, the CollectStats stat, and the ability-card clause all removed. `Part_DazeOnThrowPens.cs` kept as an ORPHAN on purpose — earmarked for a wild rock-thrower variant (cragmensch note, TODO-archive benchmarks). Companion proposals from the same item (whorl stat gains, damage curve) tracked separately below, UNRULED at time of writing. `[verify]` thrown stones no longer daze; level text and ability card show no daze line.
- [ ] `[design]` **Whorl stat shifts CUT — RULED 2026-08-06 (user: "cut stat shift from whorl") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** The reserve-size AV (+1 at 3+, +2 at 9+) and the resonant DV swap (+2/+4, the 2026-07-23 compensation ruling — superseded with its parent) are gone; `Sync` now clears any lingering shift (old-save hygiene) and the whorl's defense is deflection alone (3%/stone +1%/level, outcomes 50/30/20). Level-text AV lines and the ability-card "grants DV in place of AV" clause removed; the parked whorl-DV display fix is MOOT. `[verify]` reserve at 9+: character sheet shows no whorl AV/DV; attune on/off flips deflect type only.
- [ ] `[design]` **Throw damage curve — PEN CUT RULED 2026-08-06 (user data point: "one shotting electrofuges at level 4"; from the simmed menu: "I lean penetration cut, yeag") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** Pen bonus Lv/2 → **Lv/3** (+1/+2/+3 at 3/6/9); damage dice UNTOUCHED at (1+L/3)d6+(L%3). Sim ground truth (`design/eb_damage_sim.py` + electrofuge one-shot runs, Toughness HP modifier included per user reminder — Leveler.cs applies TouMod×(Lv−1)): P(one-shot electrofuge | hit) at L4 drops 19-37% → 8-21%; L10 avg vs AV 3 drops 44 → 29, landing at Light Manipulation lase parity (28) — defensible as "EB per-hit ≈ lase, paying the to-hit/range tax with a bigger magazine." Dice-cut and both-cut variants REJECTED (both-cut undershot lase). Rider supersession: resonant pens (trained/5, "+1 at Lv10" per the 2026-07-22 ruling) is now 0 at every level — resonant stones ride pure vibro; noted in MakeStone. Every-level lattice intact (damage bonus/dice tick all ten levels). `[verify]` L4 stones read 2d6+1 pv+1 on the sheet; electrofuges survive most single hits at L4; resonant stones show no pen bonus.
- [x] `[design]` ~~Balance pass~~ — done 2026-07-21 vs Light Manipulation (decompiled ground truth). Verdict: EB out-bursts LM ~2:1 vs soft/medium armor with a bigger magazine and daze; falls flat on reliability (to-hit + ~10 tile range vs LM's auto-hit infinite line), out-of-combat utility, and sustain. **Cost stays 4 MP.** *(daze half superseded by the 2026-08-06 cut above)*
- [x] `[design]` ~~Charge regen~~ — **implemented: Willpower-scaled**, Light Manipulation's exact formula (base 15 turns, −5%/point above WIL 16, clamped [3, 75]). Second data point in the mod's "the earth rewards the disciplined" Willpower theme (with Gorgon's Gaze DC).
- [x] `[design]` ~~Projectile protection~~ — **implemented: "Gravel Whorl"** (user design, `Effect_GravelWhorl.cs`). While toggled on with stones in reserve: deflect chance vs *physical* projectiles = 3% × current charges + 1% × level (46% at Lv10 full reserve); lasers/plasma/electric pass through (beam attacks bypass inherently — they never fire `DefenderMissileHitEvent`; energy *weapons* filtered by projectile attributes); +1 AV at 3+ stones, +2 at 9+. Deflecting costs nothing — **throwing depletes the shield**; hoard-or-hurl is the tension. Modeled on Calcify's `WhorlingStones` code shape.
- [x] `[bug]` **Resonant penetration double-dip (user playtest find 2026-07-22, RULED −5):** the level-scaled PenetrationBonus (+5 at Lv10) stacked on top of Vorpal's pen=AV matching, rolling ~6 penetrations vs ANY armor — resonant did ~60 to snapjaws vs normal stones' ~30, inverting the designed trade (resonant = flat ~23 vs everything, eb_sim.py). **Fixed in MakeStone (ruling refined twice, final):** attuned stones keep **one fifth** of the level-scaled bonus (`scaledPens / 5` — +1 at Lv10, scales at wish-levels; earlier forms −5-flat and flat-1 superseded). Covers both sculpt and in-place retune (both route through MakeStone). Level text notes the trade. `[verify]` resonant vs snapjaw ≈ 1 penetration ~10 dmg; vs Cragmensch still ÷ AV-matched.
- [x] `[design]` **Resonant stones — IMPLEMENTED 2026-07-21.** "Attune Stones" toggle granted at Lv10 (`SyncAttuneAbility`, granted/revoked on level change); resonant blueprint `Cleo_TerraFirma_EphemeralStoneResonant` (cyan, `VibroWeapon ChargeUse=0` + thrown `Vorpal`); `RetuneHeldStone()` swaps the held stone in place at net-zero charge cost (guarded against the replace-handler double-sculpt); Gravel Whorl filter flips to Light-tagged projectiles + DV +2/+3 while resonant; level text and new ability card in place. Icon is a cyan-tinted reuse of the takestones tile — swap for bespoke art whenever. Spec (for reference): A second toggle ability (gated ~Lv10, tunable) that attunes your stones: they gain the adjective **{{C|resonant}}** (cyan) and vibro penetration (PV=AV), base form/name ladder unchanged. Sim grounding (`eb_sim.py`): resonant = flat ~23 avg vs any armor — Cragmensch 1 throw, Lithofex burstable, Chrome Pyramid still no (171) — while normal stones do 68 vs AV 3; a true choice, not an upgrade.
  - **Toggling retunes the stone already in hand, in place, at no charge cost** (free action) — never wastes a stone for a player who forgets to pre-toggle; auto-equip continues sculpting whichever mode is active.
  - **The whorl retunes with the stones (locked 2026-07-21):** the toggle flips the shield's filter along with the ammo. Normal whorl: deflects physical projectiles, +1/+2 AV (as shipped). Resonant whorl: deflects **Light-tagged projectiles** (laser rifle/pistol fire — verified `Attributes="Light Laser"` in game blueprints; filter = attributes contain `Light`), physical projectiles pass through, and the AV bonus becomes **+2/+4 DV (RULED 2026-07-23, supersedes the provisional +2/+3: "DV is less valuable than AV so you need more to compensate" — the full 1 AV→2 DV rate; effect + level text updated, deployed)**. Same deflect-chance formula both modes. One toggle retunes offense and defense to the same world: mass vs energy — preserves "a choice, not an upgrade." Beam attacks (Lase-type) bypass both modes (engine: no missile event) — say so in the description text.
  - Implementation notes: melee half = `VibroWeapon ChargeUse="0"` (precedent: the mod's statue fist); thrown half = `Vorpal` attribute on `ThrownWeapon` (what the engine's ÷ penetration display keys off) — **verify the thrown path in-game**. Daze rider unchanged in both modes.
  - **Division of labor:** cleanest shape is a second blueprint (`Cleo_TerraFirma_EphemeralStoneResonant` inheriting the base stone: cyan Render colors, VibroWeapon/Vorpal, flavor description) — **user offered to own the XML**; C# side picks the blueprint by toggle state and handles the in-place retune.
  - Ties into "throw-augmenting items" (design budget) as the broader late-game answer; Chrome Pyramids stay out of reach by design. **(Provenance audit 2026-07-26: "out of reach by design" is a CLAUDE READING off eb_sim.py, UNRULED — no ruling exists that Pyramids must stay unreachable.)**
- [ ] `[design]` **Resonant whorl DV swap — FLAGGED FOR FIX SOONISH (user 2026-07-26, mid
  voice-sitting): "i realized this last bit is not doing the thing i wanted in the mutation
  itself, even though the description is accurate."** The +2/+4-DV-in-place-of-AV behavior
  (RULED 2026-07-23, "DV is less valuable than AV so you need more to compensate") matches

### Late verification-session flags (2026-07-23, recorded at session wrap)

(b) +1 fall per 2 depth steps **HELD as a future dial** — the only piece not built, by design.

Net outcome fine either way (rock spent), and "the vortex ate the stone that opened it" is on-brand — but the null-cell state during the post-throw chain is the flag-lite: if anything downstream assumes a non-null cell there (payload echoes, whorl-deflect restore), this is the seed of a future NRE. Check `FallenRockPart`'s post-throw path for null-cell tolerance at the next code sitting; no player-visible misbehavior observed.

### Whorl outcome scale — **BUILT 2026-07-22 (re-ruled same day; supersedes the 2026-07-21 spec below in three places)**
The three-stage deflection landed (`Effect_GravelWhorl.cs`: `Devour`/`Swat`/`Ricochet`, dispatched on a successful DeflectChance roll). Today's rulings vs the old spec:
- **Split RULED: even thirds (1d3)** — supersedes the old 50/30/20. **RE-RULED same day (third round): back to 50/30/20** — user asked which works better; Claude's case: rarity graded to drama (devour common = the mass hungers and feeds the identity; swat mundane middle; ricochet at 20% stays a story instead of noise). Named consequence, accepted: grenade stones now get torn apart on the defender in HALF of deflects rather than a third — the mind-game sharpens.
- **Devoured:** ephemera obliterate; **plain stones (`IsAnyStone`) feed the reserve +1 charge, capped at max** (as originally specced — enemy rock-throwers feed the barrager; "The whorl thickens." DRAFT message; `Charges++` + `SyncWhorl`, plumbing verified public). **DANGEROUS stones don't join the mass — RULED 2026-07-22 (second ruling round): they're TORN APART in the gravelly mass and their effects TRIGGER.** Boundary = the mill's own hazard class (`IGrenade` part OR `GrindSpawn` tag): grenade stones detonate **at the defender's cell** (a quarrelquartz eaten by your own whorl goes off on you — the mind game vs whorl-users is intended; blame attribution = the thrower via `E.Attacker`, so their kill not your suicide), grind-special stones release their contents around the defender (gyrestone → vortices in your lap, the mill's scatter shape). No charge gained either way. **DELTA — RULED KEPT 2026-07-23: non-stone real items are OBLITERATED** (user: "throwable items are almost always disposable… if you were throwing expensive items at an enemy's whorl to see what happens, you deserve to find out"). Rider from the same ruling, BUILT + DEPLOYED: the torn-apart branch (grenades detonate at the defender, blame to the thrower) now applies to **ALL devoured grenades, not just mod stones** — "as long as when a grenade hits the whorl, it explodes"; a base-game grenade is no longer silently defused. `[verify]` if an enemy-thrown base-game grenade can even reach the outcome scale (grenades usually target cells, not creatures).
- **Swatted:** the original behavior verbatim (munitions fizzle; real items to a random open adjacent cell).
- **Ricochet RULED (after checking the Light Manipulation precedent at user request — base reflect is ANGLE-based, `Direction = Angle + 180`, never target-picked): fully random angle line-trace** — from the defender's cell, range 5 (DRAFT), first creature on the line takes flat d20 vs DV (no marksmanship) and **the projectile's own damage** (ThrownWeapon die / Projectile BaseDamage; supersedes the old −2 PV `RollDamagePenetrations` mini-attack — simpler, no pen math); carom ends at the first body hit or flinched; walls stop it; real items land where it ends, munitions spend themselves; kill attribution = whorl's owner; **payload channels deliberately don't fire** (the whorl redirects mass, not triggers). Applies identically to resonant-mode deflects (light munitions careen too).
- [x] `[bug]` **Unequipped sculpted stones pocketed as inventory items — RULED + FIXED + DEPLOYED 2026-07-23 (active next restart):** manually unequipping an EB sculpted stone left it sitting in the pack as a possession. User ruling: "return back to the take stones option, or be destroyed" — implemented as both: at end of turn, any sculpted stone in the caster's INVENTORY is reabsorbed (charge refunded when the reserve has room, crumbles when full). Deliberately end-of-turn rather than the unequip event — throws also unequip mid-flight, and an unequip hook would refund stones actually thrown. Message "You return the stone to the earth." DRAFT. `[verify]` unequip a sculpted stone → it vanishes next turn + charge count rises; thrown stones still spend normally.
- [ ] `[design]` **Aegischist thrown = force BUBBLE around the victim, impassable (user ruling flag, 2026-07-23):** hitting an enemy should cage them in a ring of force shards **they cannot move through** — a thrown prison — replacing the current 3-cell wall line on hits (the line doesn't enclose anything; live-fire showed victims just walking away). Build shape: the thrown-hit path reuses the existing step channel's bubble recipe (ring of 8 around the target); keep the wall line for misses (terrain denial at the landing cell). Build-time check: base `Forcefield` pass semantics — ensure the VICTIM can't slip through (owner-pass rules; shards may need to be ownerless or thrower-owned), and decide whether the ring skipping occupied/solid cells leaves exploitable gaps (wall-adjacent enemies get partial cages — acceptable or fill-with-what).
- [x] `[polish]` **Whorl message conjugation typo — FIXED + DEPLOYED 2026-07-23 (active next restart):** "your gravel whorl **send** the laser beam careening". Root cause (decomp-confirmed, `Messaging.XDidY`): the calls passed BOTH the possessive string subject AND `Actor: Object` — the engine conjugates via `Actor.GetVerb`, so a player-owned whorl got second-person verbs ("send") under a third-person subject; NPC whorls read correctly by coincidence. Fix, all nine call sites / five verbs in `Effect_GravelWhorl.cs`: verbs pre-conjugated third-person ("devours"/"tears"/"deflects"/"swats"/"sends"), `Actor: Object` → `Source: Object` (Source keeps the visibility gate `UseVisibilityOf ?? Source ?? Actor` and message routing; a null-Actor with no Source would have silently dropped the messages). `[verify]` each outcome message reads correctly in-game.
- [x] `[bug?]` **Whorl-deflected THROWN items: placement drift + fragility — RULED (b) + BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-07-23.** (The find: `PerformThrow` finishes the throw AFTER Devour/Swat/Ricochet acts — re-places the weapon at its own landing cell, overriding the outcome's placement, and the deflected rock then rolled throw-fragility and broke, undercutting "loot preserved.") Ruling: mark the projectile. New transient part `Cleo_TerraFirma_WhorlDeflectedPart` — Swat/Ricochet attach it to real thrown weapons with the outcome's landing cell; at `AfterThrownEvent` it restores that placement (if the engine moved it) and removes itself; `FallenRockPart` calls its `Restore()` FIRST (so miss-payloads/deferred pulses fire from the right cell) and skips the fragility roll while marked (a deflection is the whorl's use, not the thrower's). Added at runtime = always last in the part list, so self-removal mid-dispatch is safe. `[verify]` deflected thrown rock rests where the message says + survives (no fragility break); non-rock thrown items (javelin) also rest at the outcome's cell.
- [ ] `[verify]` outcome scale: deflects split visibly three ways over a fight; devoured plain stone → charge count ticks up (capped) + message; devoured quarrelquartz → detonates ON the defender (and the death message blames the thrower); devoured gyrestone → vortices around the defender; devoured arrow simply gone; ricochet line now renders a staggered gravel-blip trace + endpoint dust puff (RULED + BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-07-23; "we could mess around with animations later on" — richer VFX parked as a future pass) — verify the trace reads as travel and hits/flinches/landings resolve, including friendly fire and return-to-sender geometry; whorl's effect description shows the new outcome text; resonant whorl still only touches Light projectiles.
- **Resonant `RefractLight` upgrade — BUILT 2026-07-22** (the 2026-07-21 decision, closed): the effect registers the `"RefractLight"` string event (BlinkingTicSickness idiom: `RegisterEffectEvent` in Apply, unregister in Remove — registered always, handler gates on Resonant since Sync flips attunement at runtime); on success (same DeflectChance) it stamps By + Verb "scatter" (DRAFT) and returns false. **Resonant whorls now scatter Lase-type beams** — the "direct beams pass through" gap closes for resonant mode (normal mode keeps it; effect description texts updated both branches, DRAFT). Two decompile findings shaped it: **(1) beams-only gating** — laser missile SHOTS fire the same event with a `Projectile` param set (MissileWeapon.cs:1426) and are excluded by the handler, because they already roll the missile-hit outcome scale (no double-dip; without the gate resonant would get two bites at every laser bolt, ~1−(1−c)²); Lase beams pass `Projectile=null` (LightManipulation.cs:159). **(2) Fully random scatter is engine-native** — both beam paths pre-seed `Direction = Stat.Random(0,359)` and the handler deliberately does NOT override it (the old spec's `RetroVariance`-style angle math turned out unnecessary — an unset Direction IS the gravel scatter). Sound = engine default `sfx_light_refract`.
- [ ] `[verify]` beam-scatter: resonant whorl + hostile Lase = beams sometimes scatter off you in random directions with the refract sound and "X scatters the laser beam!"; scattered beams can hit bystanders (engine walks the new path); normal-mode whorl never scatters beams; laser PISTOL shots still resolve through the outcome scale only (watch for double messages — the no-double-dip gate); scatter survives save/load (string-event registration across saves — the one serialization unknown).

<details><summary>Superseded 2026-07-21 spec (reference)</summary>
Eaten ~50% (real items drop at feet; stones absorb +1), Deflected ~30%, Ricochet ~20% or 10%+1%/Lv (2–4 cells, −2 PV mini-attack via RollDamagePenetrations). Physical only — the engine has no refract system for non-light projectiles (verified 2026-07-21).
</details>

### Design calls
- [ ] `[polish]` **"Rough" statue look-text claims "intricately depicts" (Discord-feedback thread 2026-08-07, a friend's catch relayed by the user; user's "might be fixed now" hunch checked — NOT fixed, the 08-06 true-copy pass only touched WALL descriptions):** "intricate" appears nowhere in our files or git history — it's hard-coded in the base `RandomStatue.SetCreature` template ("This sculpture worked from {material} intricately depicts {creature}: ..."), stamped on every carve at `MakeStatueOfCreatureFromWall`'s `SetCreature(Muse)` call. Scope = Stoneshaper carves only (Gorgon statues never enter this path; sole `SetCreature` caller checked). DeanimateStatue copies `_Short` to the inert statue, so a carve-time fix rides through expiry. **RULED + BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-08-07 (compiles green, hash-verified — active next restart): TIER-1-ONLY fix** — after the six-tier lookbox mockup sitting (full draft ladder rendered: crudely/finely/faithfully/intricately-rehomed/masterfully/flawlessly), the user chose the minimal cut ("we could go even simpler and *just* do tier 1"; Claude concurred — rough is the only tier that CONTRADICTS the base sentence, the others merely restate the name's tier signal). Rough carves now read **"crudely depicts" (user's own word, from "rough statues depicted crudely" — no draft ceremony)** via a `NamePrefix == "rough"` string replace after `SetCreature` in `MakeStatueOfCreatureFromWall`; every other tier keeps the untouched base template (maximal base-voice adherence). Name ladder untouched — display names still climb rough→immaculate. The descriptor switch is the standing hook if the full ladder ever seems worth it (draft words above are the bench). Rides into the inert statue via DeanimateStatue's `_Short` copy. `[verify]` next boot: Lv1-2 carve look-text reads "crudely depicts"; a Lv3+ carve still reads "intricately depicts"; expired rough statue keeps "crudely".
- [x] `[design]` **Distraction fossil → tank statue — RULED 2026-07-21: revive as taunt. BUILT** (uncommented, `Statue.Brain.Passive` removed). Residual watch item (polish pass): user note "hopefully not too strong" — watch in testing; immunity mechanic should self-balance.
  *(superseded text below kept for reference)* Original proposal: User sees value in a statue that draws aggro — unique among familiars. Verified 2026-07-21: the commented block still compiles against the current `Distraction` part; revival = uncomment + delete `Statue.Brain.Passive = true;`. Mechanic niceties (decompiled): a successful save grants `DistractionImmunity` for 3600–8400 turns (taunt checks each enemy once — no per-turn spam, strong-willed enemies opt out cleanly), and it never affects the player (future wild stoneshapers can't taunt-lock you). Save is Willpower vs 10+Lv — another Willpower-theme touchpoint, on the enemy's side. Options: (a) delete fossil; **(c) revive as taunt — recommended**; statue stats (25 HP/Lv, wall AV, modest damage) already profile as a tank.
- [ ] `[design]` `[verify]` Ensure it feels balanced and actually copies the creature's anatomy (`Body.Rebuild(Creature.Body.Anatomy)` — API verified real; test in-game against weird body plans: Slog, swarms, no-handed creatures).
- **Balance rationale (user, 2026-07-21):** statues are deliberately weaker than Beguiling — temporary by design, copies without inventory; "a survivable buddy who helps with a couple fights." Recorded as the design intent.
- [x] `[design]` ~~**Open tension with the above:** level 10 currently grants a *permanent* statue (until destroyed). Does the "naturally de-animates, inherently temporary" intent override the Lv10 permanence capstone, or is permanence the intended Lv10 exception? User ruling needed.~~ **ANSWERED by the Lv10-permanence ruling two entries below (kept as capstone) — this note predated it.**
- [x] `[design]` **Natural weapons — RULED 2026-07-21: cosmetic middle ground. BUILT** (and repaired live 2026-07-23 — see "Raised in live-fire" below). Statue natural weapons copy the muse's weapon **tile, name, and slot only**; damage/to-hit stay on the mutation's level formula (`GetNaturalDamage`/`GetToHitBonus`). Implement in `ApplyStatueBuffs`: when the replaced part had a DefaultBehavior, copy its Render tile + DisplayName onto the haymaker instance; fall back to gravelly haymaker cosmetics when it had none. (Survey verdict that killed wholesale-keep: disease/poison/tech-organ leaks — see below.)
- [x] `[design]` **Lv10 permanence — RULED 2026-07-21: kept as capstone.** Rewards investment; duration ceasing to matter past 10 is accepted.
- [x] `[bug]` `[design]` **Inert container statue — RULED 2026-07-21, unified spec. BUILT** (all three paths; in-game verification tracked in the checklist at the bottom). One shared helper: *render a creature down into an inert, lootable statue* (fresh non-animated statue object with the creature's render/name/description; all possessions — inventory + equipped — transferred into it; lootable via Inventory + the base game's container part — verify exact part name against e.g. the Chest blueprint at implementation; contingency if containers prove hard: drop a gear pile beside the statue). Serves three paths:
  1. ✔ *Gorgon petrify* (kill path) — **implemented 2026-07-21:** shared helper `_StatueHelpers.cs` (`EntombPossessions`/`ReturnPossessions`, Chest-verified Container+Inventory recipe, all APIs precedent-checked); `Petrify()` entombs before `Die()` and unwinds if the death is prevented.
  2. *Stoneshaper natural expiry (Lv 1–9):* statue no longer reverts to a wall — it freezes into an inert container statue where it stands, lent gear entombed but recoverable. Wall-restore code and `CurWallBp` bookkeeping retire. **Accepted cost: consumed walls never come back.** Fixes the equipped-gear-obliterated bug by design.
  3. *Permanent-statue swap (Lv 10+) — NEW, ruled:* casting with a living permanent statue no longer blocked; the old statue de-animates via the same helper (gear entombed, lootable) and the new one is carved. Normal `BASE_COOLDOWN` applies per cast. Temporary statues (Lv 1–9) remain un-swappable — the lifetime-as-cooldown display already gates recasting naturally.
  4. *Swap confirmation (ruled 2026-07-21):* player-only `Popup.ShowYesNo` before a swap that de-animates a living permanent statue, naming the statue in the prompt (Sunder Mind's self-target confirm is the house precedent). Extra stakes: permanent statues keep their `Leveler` part — they gain levels over time, which a swap destroys unrecoverably; say so in the prompt wording if it fits.

## Calcify

*(resolved -> TODO-archive.md: Small fixes)*

### Design calls
- [x] `[design]` **Whorling stones — RULED 2026-07-21: CUT entirely.** Deflection is now exclusively Earthen Barrage's identity (Gravel Whorl); Calcify is purely the wall mutation. Implemented: `Effect_WhorlingStones.cs` deleted; Calcify's `AssocEffect` field, `EndTurnEvent` handler, deflect helper/const, and level-text lines removed. Bonus cleanup: Calcify's vestigial unused `IModEventHandler<AfterSuccessfulThrowEvent>` interface + using removed (would have broken the de-Harmony batch).
- [ ] `[design]` **Balance target — RULED: par with Force Wall, a bit stronger for the investment.** Force Wall ground truth (decompiled): mental, 3 MP, 9 forcefields fixed at all levels, duration 14+2×Lv (34 at 10), cooldown 100, owner can shoot missiles OUT through it. Calcify: physical, 2 MP, 3+Lv/2 walls (3→8), **permanent**, 500×ceil(Lv/2) HP, cooldown 90, opaque (blocks own missiles too), adjacent-start placement only, Stoneshaper can carve its walls. Assessment: Calcify is count-weaker early, decisively stronger late (permanence + HP scale). **RULED + implemented: `BASE_WALLS_CREATED` 3 → 4** (4 at Lv1, exactly 9 at Lv10 — matching Force Wall's count at full investment, in permanent stone). Otherwise numbers stand.
- [x] `[bug]` **MOOTED by the 2026-08-06 true-copy rebalance (next entry): walls now keep native AV/DV and the level text claims neither.** ~~Calcify level text vs wall reality~~ (user flag 2026-07-24, verify session: "calcify description is inaccurate - spawned walls have -10 DV. 0 AV as expected, though this makes them substantially weaker than base walls (10 AV). not sure if i want to flag this as a bug or intended").** Recon split: **AV 0 is INTENDED and stated** (level text: "Created walls have 0 AV and 0 DV"; the design trades armor for the scaled HP pool, 500×⌈Lv/2⌉ — weaker than base walls on purpose; the HP is the defense). **The DV half is a real mismatch:** the text promises 0 DV but created walls keep the base wall blueprint's universal −10 DV (nothing overrides it). Fix either way at the batch: (a) override DV→0 at creation to honor the promise, or (b) correct the text to −10 (every wall in Qud is −10; "0 DV" was always an odd promise for a wall). Claude lean: (b), text follows reality.
- [ ] `[design]` **Fugue-clone Calcify walls outlive the clone (user live-fire flag 2026-07-24, verify session 2: "temporal fugue calcify walls remain after the fugue clone dissipates") — RECON DONE, BUILD SHAPE READY, awaiting ruling.** As-is, a temporary clone leaves PERMANENT walls (free wall economy from a duration ability; also fires via the blurring rock's fugue clone). Decomp-verified build shape: fugue clones carry the `FugueCopy` string property (`TemporalFugue.CreateFugueCopyOf`, line 299); Calcify's wall placement checks `ParentObject.HasStringProperty("FugueCopy")` and, when true, attaches the engine's own `ExistenceSupport` part (`SupportedBy` = the casting clone, `ValidateEveryTurn` = true) to each wall — walls dissipate the moment their maker does, normal casts untouched. Options if ruled differently: Temporary-with-duration instead (walls linger a fixed time after the clone), or keep persistence as-is (Qud-weird freebie). Claude lean: ExistenceSupport — "walls as temporal as their maker." **RULED the lean 2026-08-05 (user, beta-schedule review: "sounds fine") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day (compiles clean, hash-verified — active next restart):** `PlaceWall` in Mutation_Calcify.cs checks the caster for the engine's `FugueCopy` string property and attaches `ExistenceSupport` (SupportedBy = the clone, ValidateEveryTurn) to each wall it raises — clone dissipates, walls follow; normal casts untouched; the blurring rock's fugue clone rides the same property for free. CALC cast log gains "fugue-tethered" when it fires. `[verify]` Friday boot: fugue-clone a Calcify caster, have the clone wall off, watch the walls vanish when the clone expires; a normal cast's walls persist as ever.
- [x] `[design]` **NPC-caster gap — RULED batch-now + BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-07-23** (user un-parked it: "let's knock calcify off the list"). Two halves: (1) AI advertisement on `AIGetDefensiveAbilityListEvent` + `AIGetRetreatAbilityListEvent` ONLY — deliberately never offensive, because calcified stone is opaque both ways (Force Wall advertises offensively only because its owner can shoot out through its own fields); threat = the event's target, else Force Wall's nearest-visible-hostile retreat scan, passed through `E.Add` so the command carries it. (2) NPC cell-picking `PickNpcWallLine`: perpendicular line across the approach direction, anchored at the caster's adjacent cell (honoring the walls-start-adjacent rule), extended along both orthogonals to the wall count; occupied cells skipped by the placer — partial lines accepted like partial player casts. `[verify]` wish Calcify onto a creature and watch it wall off its attacker when pressed (the appendix "NPC Fissure/Gorgon casters" item gains a calcifier sibling).
- [ ] `[design]` **True-copy walls — RULED 2026-08-06 (beta rebalance pass, item 1; user: "calcify should fully copy the wall it's adjacent to if possible, including description and stats. it should no longer gain HP scaling per level") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** Walls are now full copies of their source blueprint: native HP, AV, DV, and description all stand; level buys count only (4 → 9). Supersedes: the 500×⌈Lv/2⌉ HP formula (`GetWallHp` retired), the 2026-07-25 0-AV/-10-DV ratification (walls keep real armor now), and the HP clause of the par-with-Force-Wall ruling above (count parity at Lv10 stands; the defense dial moved from level to geology — sandstone 150 HP/AV 8, shale 200/10, limestone 1000/15, slate 3000/25, granite 26000/40). `Cleo_TerraFirma_CalcifyWallPart` reduced to a marker (the "calcified" name prefix + the fugue-tether anchor); its shift fields survive for pre-rebalance saves. Fallback blueprint (no natural stone anywhere) rides its Sandstone inherit, 150 HP/AV 8, and carries the old bespoke description in XML now — CLAUDE DIAL, flag if the floor feels wrong. New strings DRAFT for signoff: level-text line "Walls are true copies of the local stone" and ability-card sentence "Creates N walls, true copies of the local stone." `[verify]` cast beside shale and beside limestone: look shows the source stone's description + real stats under the "calcified" prefix; fallback zone shows gray stone at 150 HP.
- [ ] `[design]` **Every-level lattice + scaling cooldown — RULED 2026-08-06 (user flag: "I need to make sure every level up increases something on the mutations, including on calcify"; on the cooldown proposal: "we can try out cool down") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** True-copy left Calcify's odd levels dead (walls tick on evens only; the retired HP formula had covered odds), and the audit confirmed it was the ONLY leveled mutation with dead levels (Gorgon/EB/Stoneshaper/Fissure all move something every level). Fix: cooldown now `max(100 − 10×⌈Lv/2⌉, 50)` — 90 at Lv1-2 (Lv1 unchanged), then 80/70/60/50 stepping on each odd level; evens keep buying the wall. Base precedent for declining cooldowns, decomp-verified: Burgeoning 115−10L, Teleport Other 115−10L, Belcher 210−10L, Sticky Tongue 22−L, Burrowing 15−L. The old "permanent product ⇒ flat cooldown" line was a CLAUDE recommendation (redoubt-numbers-draft) premised on level buying wall HP — premise dissolved with true-copy, provenance checked before superseding. Level text cooldown line now rules-wrapped (it scales). Framed as TRY-OUT — feel-session revisit expected. **Companion ruling, general (user): "1 cost and defect mutations don't level" — Geomancy and Lithovore exempt from the every-level rule by design.** `[verify]` level a calcifier 1→10: ability card cooldown reads 90/90/80/80/70/70/60/60/50/50 while wall count reads 4/5/5/6/6/7/7/8/8/9.

- [ ] `[design]` **Fixed radius, steeper damage/stun — RULED 2026-08-06 (beta rebalance pass, item 2; user: "instead of fissure range expanding with level, it could get a bit more damage and stun increment"; radius 4 + ladder proposal: "sounds fine") + BUILT + DEPLOYED same day.** Ring radius is now a FLAT 4 at every level (was 2/3/4/5 by band; `MAX_RING_RADIUS` replaced by `RING_RADIUS = 4`) — the scaling budget moved into the ladders: damage 1d3 +1/+3/+5/+7 by band (was +1/+2/+3/+5; 1d3 base kept deliberately, rider-damage family per Stunning Force's grammar, tight variance for an unmitigated disk-wide hit — user probed "why not 3d3," reasoning accepted) and stun 1/2/3/4 rounds by band (was 1/2/2/3; band 4's 4 rounds edges past Stunning Force's flat 3, paid for by the moved-targets-only nuance, the scaling Toughness DC, and double the cooldown). Band-1 damage/stun unchanged: the radius jump 2→4 is already the early-level gift (side effect accepted and noted in code). Carve depth still 1/2/3/full-radius by band; per-level dials (carve PV 6+3L, stun DC 16+L, push (2+L)×1000) untouched, so the every-level lattice holds. Level-text radius line unwrapped (flat constants go bare). Supersedes the redoubt-numbers-draft radius/damage/stun tables. `[verify]` L1 cast: radius-4 ring, 6 gaps, mostly rubble; L10 cast: same footprint, 3 gaps, walls, 1d3+7 hits and 4-round stuns on movers.

- **Part_FallenRock.cs additions:** three payload channels — `Effect` (Blind/Luminous/Mutating by name, amount = duration), `Water` (Stomach's native `AddWater` event; 10000 = one drink), `RandomGas` (the aji conch's exact roll: `PopulationManager.GenerateOne`, default table `DynamicObjectsTable:AjiConch`, conch's seeping rule copied) — plus `StepPayload`/`StepPayloadDetail`/`StepPayloadAmount` override fields for rocks whose step behavior differs in kind (used as-given, never halved; SameAs updated).
- **Rocks.xml:** fungal rock ×4 (band 2; Puff/Luminous/Wax/Mumbles spore gas, color-variant tints are drafts), compressed ash (band 2; MiasmaticAsh), chunk of salt (band 1; thrown = AddWater 10000 on victim, step = native `SaltPuddle`), warped ajistone (band 3; RandomGas 40), vantabsidian (band 3; `Blackout` Radius 5 field, thrown Blind 20, StepChance 0 = inert underfoot), glowing twins (band 3; identical render+description both — radioactive: PoisonGas 40 thrown + Mutating 100; phosphorescent: Luminous 300; **deviation flagged in XML comment: phosphorescent got a 25% Luminous step proc the catalog text didn't specify, so the twins' underfoot/pathfinding behavior is identical — no tell**).
- **Populations.xml:** Band2New += fungal group (9, split 4 ways) + ash (9); Band3New += ajistone (8) + vantabsidian (8) + glowing group (7, twins 50/50); Rocks1 += salt (10). All three XMLs re-validated well-formed.

Spiderweb's web count (3) and step-Stuck 12 are Claude numbers flagged for ratification; melting follows house liquid-rock numbers.

Stalactite item: thrown 1d6/pen 6 ShortBlades + Effect channel ShatteredArmor (−1 AV, 100 rds) on hit, inert underfoot. Stalagmite: Solid/Flyover/AllowMissiles per BaseStalagmite conventions, crush 2d6; `DEBRIS_BLUEPRINT` generalized to per-blueprint `Cleo_TerraFirma_DebrisBlueprint` tag so a stalagmite that fails to kill shatters into stalactites, not generic rocks.

- **Baboon figurine (#21) recon — 2026-07-22, decompiled ground truth; design discussion open, NOTHING BUILT:**
  - **Holdable creature: engine-impossible.** `ActionManager.ValidateActor` (decompiled) removes any actor whose `CurrentCell` is null from the action queue AND logs an "ActionQueueInconsistency" engine error — a creature inside an inventory structurally cannot take turns. Only item↔creature *transformation* exists (turret-deploy pattern), which doesn't act while held. Dead end for "sentient held figure" as a literal creature.
  - **Item that attacks autonomously while held: fully feasible, strong precedent.** The **Point-Defense Drone** (Items.xml:6736) is an equippable item carrying its own `MissileWeapon` (`FiresManually="false"`, `EnergyCost="0"`, `NoWildfire="true"`) + `EnergyAmmoLoader` + a `PointDefense` part that fires it autonomously via `Event "CommandFireMissile"` with `Owner`=equipper, `EnergyMultiplier` 0f (zero turn cost to the holder). `CommandFireMissile` verified: takes Actor/Owner + TargetCell, StartCell defaults to owner's cell, no creature-firer assumption. New code = ONE small targeting part (PointDefense's shape minus interception): TurnTick while equipped → nearest visible hostile-to-holder in range → path-clear check (PointDefense.PathClear is the recipe) → fire. Renders as the figurine hurling a stone.
  - **`AddsRep` verified** (decompiled): `WorksOnEquipper` default true — held in hand = equipped = rep applies; `WorksOnCarrier="true"` XML flag available for mere-inventory rep (Taken/Dropped handled). Player-only by design. Neck-ring precedent = `Blood-stained neck-ring`, `Faction="Baboons" Value="400"`.
  - **No native stone projectile blueprint** — define `Cleo_TerraFirma_ProjectileFigurineStone` (`TemporaryProjectile` child, Render + `Projectile BasePenetration/BaseDamage`, ~ProjectileStoneGaze shape). Trivial XML.
  - **RULED 2026-07-22, then PARKED (user: park it; spec recorded, nothing built).** Rulings: plain-stone shots (no rock-table chaos); active in **hand, thrown slot, or on the ground** (throw the figurine itself = floor sentry); transforming-pet idea EARMARKED as a separate later concept. **Parked spec (all numbers Claude drafts, unratified):** band-2 fall tables only, weight 6, NOT in ThrowableRocks, no IsAnyStone tag, not a BaseFallenRock child (no fragility/decay), crush 1+Level; AddsRep Baboons 400 (equipped); own MissileWeapon (FiresManually=false, EnergyCost=0, NoWildfire, WeaponAccuracy 12) + EnergyAmmoLoader ChargeUse=0 + new targeting part — 50%/turn fire chance, range 8, projectile 1d4/pen 4, nearest visible hostile of OWNER (last equipper/carrier; never-held = dormant), zero holder energy; enemy pickup = serves them (rocks don't discriminate); MissileWeapon Skill quirk noted (holder's matching skill aims it slightly better); Commerce ~50 unratified.
- **Fissure disk rework (2026-07-22, playtest round 2 — full user sign-off after design discussion):**
  - **Problem diagnosed:** exactly-R ring left a growing safe donut hole (adjacent enemies unhittable at rank 2+) + uniform placement decayed point-blank odds with rank + band-1 crush too light to kill an adjacent snapjaw on a 100-turn cooldown.
  - **Geometry: disk** (all open cells 1..R, caster's cell never), `GetRingCells`→`GetDiskCells`.
  - **Placement: center-weighted random** — cells weighted (R+1−d)², no enemy-seeking ("the earth doesn't aim, it falls hardest where it was shaken hardest"; user explicitly rejected aiming — quantity + honest misses). With ladder below, adjacent-cell odds ≈ 75/58/60/62% by rank — RISING with investment.
  - **Falls ladder: 6/10/16/24** (lookup by radius; was 3R+1).
  - **Crush dice doubled:** 1d4→2d4 (×9 rocks), 1d6→2d6 (×15), rubble+stalagmite 2d6→3d6, crumbled 4d6→5d6; pebbles keep 1, wall keeps 8d8+instakill. Verified tally: 1×1, 2d4×9, 2d6×15, 3d6×2, 5d6×1, 8d8×1.
  - **Proximity damage (user concept): +1d6 crush per step inside the rim** (rim +0; spill past the rim clamps to 0). Lv10 wall at distance 1 = 8d8+10+3d6 + 15% instakill.
  - **Anti-hoarding decay (user concept, 500 turns ruled):** rocks carried (inventory OR equipped) for 500 cumulative turns crumble in place to plain fallen rock (stack count preserved, player message). Floor rocks never age. Plain rock + pebbles exempt (DullAfter=0 — they ARE the dull state). Known accepted residuals: floor-caching restarts the clock; long-lived active-zone baboons end up with dull rocks; stack merges share the receiving clock. The dial: `DullAfter` on the base blueprint.
  - Level text updated (proximity line, decay line, disk wording).
  - [ ] `[verify]` disk rework: adjacent enemies get hit at EVERY rank (esp. 4+, the old donut); rocks visibly cluster near the caster; Lv1 adjacent snapjaw dies more often than not; proximity: same rock type hits harder at distance 1 than at the rim (watch numbers); carried rocks crumble at ~500 turns held with message, floor stockpiles don't age; T2-stone examination parked as the next design pass.
- **Playtest round 1 (2026-07-22, user live-fire) — 7 findings, all addressed same session:**
  1. Gas volume: all gas rocks (whorl/torpor/fungal/ash/radioactive/ajistone) Density 40→**80** thrown, step procs at half (40).
  2. **Misses now go off where they land** (user ruling, systemic): the part handles `AfterThrownEvent` — if the throw-hit payload didn't fire (FiredOnHit flag), it fires at the landing cell with a null victim (cell payloads work: webs/gas/strange; victim-effects no-op). Fixes spiderweb-miss-wastes-webs specifically.
  3. **BUG confirmed + fixed — glowing radioactive ate its Mutating:** GasGrenade sat before the mod part in the blueprint; grenades detonate-and-destroy on impact, so the part's ProjectileHit never ran. Part reordered above the grenade (comment in XML warns).
  4. Russet/blue pebble variants CUT (blueprints + both tables; pebbles weight 19→25).
  5. Hypersonic true never-miss: on real throws (TargetCell set — display queries excluded) the rock **lends the thrower `CloseThrowRangeAccuracyBonus` 100** (only if they lack both accuracy properties), raises event Range to the throw distance, and takes the lend back on ProjectileHit/AfterThrown. Residual: only the Agility hit-budget roll remains engine-locked. Tiny leak window if a throw aborts between profile and landing; stale lend also cleaned on next use.
  6. Shunting: amount 12→**16**, and the Teleport channel now passes MinDistance = half — lands 8–16 tiles away, never a 1-tile fizzle.
  7. **Fragility (user ruling, systemic): every rock risks itself per use** — completed throw or triggered step payload — surviving on **HoldChance 25** (rolled fresh each use, lucky stones chain). Wired via a bare part line on `Cleo_TerraFirma_BaseFallenRock` (children merge over it). Grenade-carrying rocks consume themselves on throw (no double roll); ConsumedOnStep (hypersonic) overrides on step; walls/rubble/stalagmite/creatures unaffected (not BaseFallenRock children). **HoldChance 25 is a Claude number — the single dial lives on the base blueprint.**
  8. **Kills ate on-hit payloads (user find):** the part listened on `ThrownProjectileHit`, which the engine fires AFTER damage — a killed defender fails validation, payload skipped. Switched to **`WeaponThrowHit`** (pre-damage, ModDisplacer's channel — why displacers work on killing blows): effects now land on the victim in the instant before death; the landing-cell fallback covers everything else. `ProjectileHit` kept for future launcher compat.
  - [ ] `[verify]` round-1 fixes: kill a weak target with a payload rock → effect fires (gas bursts, webs spawn) at their cell; bigger clouds; spiderweb miss webs the landing cell; radioactive mutates (mutant target) AND gasses; hypersonic hits reliably at low Agility (check no stray CloseThrowRangeAccuracyBonus lingers on the sheet after throws — the lend must vanish); shunting sends 8–16; rocks break ~75% per use, survivors keep working; step-triggered rocks break too; EB pebbles/Small Stones unaffected by fragility (no FallenRockPart).

  - **Lingering terrain is nearly free:** `LavaPuddle`/`SmallLavaPuddle`/`LavaPool` and `ConvalessencePuddle`/`SmallConvalessencePuddle`/`ConvalessencePool` all exist in PhysicalPhenomena.xml; the mod's `Liquid`/`SpawnObject` channels already spawn puddle blueprints (melting rock/salt precedent). Acid pools already in use. Fire/ice/acid patches = XML config on shipped channels.
  - **Deep galvena's surge-turns has NO native part** (`DischargeOnStep` is step-triggered w/ cooldown, not timed) — needs one small mod part: a temporary surge-emitter (TurnTick countdown + the mod's existing Discharge call shape, ~30 lines).
  - **Deep vitriolite's gear-break:** `XRL.World.Effects.Broken` confirmed as Rusted's sibling — reuse oxidian's `RustGear` channel recipe with Broken (new channel or a flag on RustGear).
  - **Item temperature does NOT harm the holder** (Physics.UpdateTemperature decompiled: radiant `TemperatureChange` to adjacent cells only when `CurrentCell != null`; carried/equipped items just cool toward ambient). So catalog's "very high/low base temperature" gives: floor radiance (hot rock warms/ignites neighbors each turn), native AI avoidance (any object with `Temperature > 200` emits avoidance nav-weight, decompiled Physics nav handler), item-side flame/brittle thresholds — but the #55 self-harm rule needs a mod-side mechanism in every case. Candidate shapes: on-throw typed self-damage to the thrower (we own the hook, trivial) and/or periodic sting while equipped (part TurnTick, equipped items tick — AddsRep precedent).
  - **Spawn-location options for the ruling** (combinable): (a) true fourth band table (`FissureRocksBand4`) reached by the existing `Level + (Z−10)/5` band math; (b) hard depth gate — deep stones cannot appear above some Z regardless of level; (c) escalation-exclusive — only via the ~4.8% Deeper rolls out of band 3 (rare jackpots, any depth).

### Caromcobble — NEW rock #56, the rubber rock (user concept 2026-07-22 "a rubber rock that bounces"; ALL RULED + BUILT same day)
- **Rulings, in order:** thrown shape = **ricochet throw only** (swat-skitter and step-bounce shapes declined); cooking = **`rubberLowTier`** (base parity with congealed rubbergum — extra jump, electric resist, reduced fall damage, jump-twice); band = Claude slots it → **band 2** (utility-comedy dice); **bounce on miss too** ("the rock never lands where you aimed, ever" — supersedes the specced hit-only bounce); eaten = **the hop** (involuntary harmless bounce to a random open cell within 2); name = **caromcobble** (slate first pick: carom, the billiards bounce × cobble; second slate in a row to land first pick).
- **Build:** blueprint `Cleo_TerraFirma_Rock_Rubber` (band 2: ThrownWeapon 1d4/pen 3, CrushDamage 2d4, **HoldChance 100 — rubber does not break, it bounces**, StepChance 0); new **`Payload="Ricochet"` channel** in `Part_FallenRock.cs` (generic — any future bouncy thing can carry it): fires via the lodestone deferred-pulse pattern (payload marks pending, `AfterThrownEvent` executes with the rock at rest, and the existing miss-fires-at-landing rail covers bounce-on-miss); **2 legs** (PayloadAmount), each a fully random angle line-trace (the whorl's carom shape) up to **4 cells** (RICOCHET_LEG_RANGE), first creature on a leg = flat d20 vs DV + **1d4** (PayloadDetail; attribution = thrower), leg ends at first body or wall, rock travels with it (DirectMoveTo; a chasm mid-bounce ends the flight honestly), bounce-sound per leg. Eaten hop = new `EatenHop` channel in the Lithovore (radius tag, `GetAdjacentCells(2)`, "You bounce." - [x] `[bug]` **Anti-hoarding decay duplicated rocks on stacks — CAUGHT BY LOG-WATCH + FIXED + DEPLOYED 2026-07-22 (active next restart):** the crumble handler minted the plain-rock replacement at FULL stack count but removed the source with `Destroy`, which the engine's `Stacker` intercepts on stacks (decompiled `BeforeDestroyObjectEvent`: non-Obliterate destroy on StackCount > 1 = decrement one + cancel). Net: a carried stack of N crumbled N turns in a row, minting N+(N−1)+…+1 = N(N+1)/2 plain rocks (log showed the x3/x2/x1 staircase across every carried type at turns 500/501/502). Fix: `Obliterate` instead of `Destroy` (bypasses the stack decrement by design). Audited the mod's other Destroy sites — all pre-split to single rocks or WANT one-off-the-stack semantics (step-trigger breaking one of a floor stack is correct); decay was the only minted-replacement case. Side observation for the text/design pass: shattered stalactites also decay to plain rock (they're BaseFallenRock children, never exempted) — a spear crumbling into a round rock reads a little odd; exempt via `DullAfter="0"` if unwanted. **All three follow-ups RULED + BUILT + DEPLOYED same session:** (1) stalactites exempted (`DullAfter="0"`); (2) **dull clock re-ruled 1000–2500** (was flat 500) — implemented as a per-rock expiry rolled once between `DullAfter`/`DullAfterMax` on the first carried turn, so hoards stagger instead of expiring as one message wall (roll stored in `DullAt`, excluded from SameAs like HeldTurns — stack merges share the receiving roll, same accepted fuzz); (3) **decay product re-ruled: pebbles** (weight 0) instead of plain rock — a crumbled hoard costs no carry weight, same flour/half-snack utility ("functionally the same end result" — user). Active next restart.

- [ ] `[bug]` **Fungal lottery permutation NOT persisted across save/load (caught 2026-07-24 by cross-reload log diff):** the same save stamped `permutation=0321` all pre-restart session, then `2031` after reload — per-session consistency works, per-SAVE persistence doesn't, violating the ruled contract ("random per run, consistent within the run" — the run is the save). Consequence: every reload reshuffles which fungal rock carries which infection — a player who learned their run's mapping loses it silently. Fix shape (post-session batch): store the permutation in game state (`XRLCore.Core.Game.SetStringGameState` / GetStringGameState idiom) rolled once per new game, instead of whatever session-seeded derivation `Part_FungalLottery` uses now. **RESOLVED 2026-07-25: that storage was already in place and WORKS — the tfspore experiment held 3201 across save-quit-reload ×3. The observed reshuffles were unsaved harness sessions, not a persistence failure. CLOSED.**
- [ ] **Baboon theories RECONNED (user, 2026-07-24: "1) baboons only throw 1 stone, not their full inventory 2) baboons inventories are being replaced instead of merged, so they have only mod stones"):** **Theory 2 FALSE** — base `Baboon` carries `Small Stone ×5` and the live trail shows both base Small Stone pockets AND mod rocks on the same baboons simultaneously; `Load="Merge"` inventoryobject lines are additive, confirmed by observation. **Theory 1 essentially TRUE, and it's BASE-game behavior:** decompiled `AIThrowAndScoot` only pushes a 1d3 `Flee` goal after a throw — it never initiates throws or re-readies; throwing is the brain's generic equipped-thrown-weapon attack, and nothing re-arms the slot promptly after a throw, so bite becomes the default once adjacent. The re-readies in the trail were opportunistic brain re-equips, not a cycle. (Also explains the scoot-gate mystery: Flee ≠ MoveTo, so our gate never saw it.) **FLAGGED FOR THE SCAV FIXING PASS (user 2026-07-24: "flag theory ones solution as something for the scav fixing pass"):** extend `StoneScavenger` to also re-ready from pockets when the slot is empty (call `Brain.PerformReequip` in the still-has-pocket-ammo branch) — the part's job generalizes from "fetch when dry" to "keep the thrower throwing." Build alongside whatever the dry-fetch verdict demands; one pass covers both halves of the thrower-uptime problem.
  **ESCALATION — VANILLA CONTROL RUN (user 2026-07-24: "test from base game confirms - baboons throw more stones without mods than with"):** the mod is suppressing baboon throw cadence BELOW vanilla baseline — this is no longer "base behavior we might improve" but a regression to find. Mechanism candidates for the pass, in suspicion order: **(H1) mixed-inventory re-ready failure** — baboon packs now hold mod rocks alongside Small Stones; if the brain's `PerformReequip` pick lands on a mod rock it then declines to ready/throw, the re-ready may abort and strand the slot empty (matches the long slot-EMPTY-with-full-pockets stretches in the transition trail); **(H2) the global stone merges** — `StoneMill` part + IsAnyStone tag now sit on EVERY stone including base Small Stones; an added part may change the AI's equip/throw scoring for the ammo itself; **(H3) StoneScavenger's own BeforeTakeAction presence** (passive on paper, but it's the part we added to the brain's turn). Differential experiments: vanilla + ONLY the scavenger merge (isolates H3); mod minus pocket merges (isolates H1); watch which rocks armed baboons actually throw vs sit on (H1/H2 tell). The transition trail already instruments the observation side. Fix must restore ≥vanilla cadence, not just add the pocket re-ready on top of a broken pick. **CLOSURE DATUM (2026-07-24, session's end): the fetch itself FIRED live** — a wild Baboon reached true dry (slot EMPTY, pockets 0) and the scavenger detoured to a loose stone at d=1 with the MoveTo push visible in the trail. The part's own logic is proven sound; the pass's work is purely the cadence regression + making the fetch reachable more often.
- [ ] **Scavenger experiment interim (2026-07-24, snapshots across two windows):** dominant gate is "**slot already armed**" — and not just pockets: plain wild Baboons are armed with MOD rocks they picked up themselves (pebbles/generic/pointy/salt/drifting across the population), so rocks flow into baboon hands fine without our part. Second gate: "still has pocket ammo (Small Stone)" (Hulking, correct stand-down). MoveTo/scoot gate: seen ONCE — not the starver. **Emerging picture: the fetch is a rare-tail behavior because baboons are almost never both unarmed AND empty — the user-visible "they don't scavenge" is mostly the armed steady-state. The decisive observation still owed: one baboon throwing itself completely dry, then the gate lines around it.** (Bonus sighting: a Hulking Baboon stepped on drifting rock and got Phased 15 — the reduced step proc working on NPCs.)
- **Confirmations banked this session (sheet updated):** NPC calcifier walls (log half) · fungal lottery consistency (same permutation re-stamped) · caromcobble full throw loop · staggered hoard decay (17+ crumbles, all in band) · geyser table variety + deep-stone sting/holdburn self-nicks (incl. an NPC burning holding deep vitriolite) · all three liquid-rock puddle children spawned (brine/slime/oil) incl. the miss-fires-at-landing rail · shattered-stalactite etch · farflint shunt on the victim · gyrestone vortex (with the null-cell flag-lite above).

### Stonefrog — NEW unique creature (user concept 2026-07-22; recon DONE, numbers drafted, SETTLE NEXT SESSION — nothing built)
**Concept (user):** not a statue of an existing creature — an original stonefrog. Frog's anatomy, looks like a rock, jumps to attack enemies. **Ruled so far:** role = **BOTH** fall-table ally (DropRock brain-branch, joins ape/statuette) AND wild cave hazard (dynamic encounter tables); **tongue = fully OUT** (ceiling-tongue outside engine without abstraction — user's own call; Sticky Tongue mutation + tongue lash exist but are "Very invasive" for a monster like this — user hesitant, skipped).
**Recon (decompiled/grepped, all verified):** `Frog` anatomy exists (Bodies.xml:349); `BaseFrog` (Str 12/Agi 16/Tou 12, DarkVision, ColdResistance −25, Frogs faction, full frog VO) with family anchors — ice frog L12/HP30/AV3 (+1d10 bite), svardym (jump+charge kit), Frog Golem (constructed-frog precedent). **Jump-attacks are native AI:** `Acrobatics_Jump` skill answers AIGetMovement/Offensive/RetreatAbilityList — two XML lines buy leap-at/leap-in/leap-away; svardym adds `Tactics_Charge` for the pounce. **Disguise depths:** (a) flavor-only render, XML; (b) **stone camouflage — Claude recommends**: `FoliageCamouflage`/`ICamouflage` pattern re-keyed to the mod's `IsAnyStone` tag → +DV while among rocks (serves BOTH roles: ally hides in your fissure fallout, wild one lurks in cave rubble), C#-small (one effect + trigger); (c) true ambush mimic (`Hidden`/`HiddenRender`, renders as an inert rock until it strikes) — most work, serves only the wild role, needs deeper recon. **DISGUISE DEPTH UNRULED.**
**Numbers draft (ALL Claude, unratified):** band 2 weight 5 (statuette's neighborhood); L8 / HP 25 / AV 5 (frog ~3 + stone-body +2); stone rules applied per standing ruling (AcidResistance −50, NoBleed); **cold weakness removed** (BaseFrog −25 is flesh-frog; stone doesn't freeze — flag); kit = Acrobatics_Jump + Tactics_Charge; stone bite 1d6 (stone-mimicry rule); camo +2 DV (foliage-camo's own level); **no MentalShield** (living stone animal, not animated statue — beguilable/dominatable, flagged for veto); **corpse = 1 plain fallen rock** (stalagmite Corpse-drop pattern — kill the stonefrog, throw the stonefrog); wild spawn via cave-tier dynamic tables (exact table names to verify at build); VO mix = frog idle/attack + rock hurt/death (croaks like a frog, chips like a stone).
**To settle next session:** disguise depth (a/b/c) · numbers sign-off · MentalShield call · corpse-rock call · cold-weakness call · which dynamic tables + wild rarity.

## Cooking effects  ← opened 2026-07-22 (user request; engine recon DONE, proposal drafted, awaiting rulings)

**The ask (user, 2026-07-22):** add cooking effects to the mod. Ties directly into the already-ratified Fissure extensibility note ("cooking-ingredient wall growths could spawn from the fall table") — this section supersedes that one-liner as the cooking workstream.

*(resolved -> TODO-archive.md: Cooking-system ground truth (decompiled + base XML, 2026-07-... / Meal-generation ground truth (decompiled 2026-07-22 second d...)*

### Direction RULED 2026-07-22: mutation-themed effects, not rock-domain tagging
**⇒ PASS STRUCTURE RE-RULED later same day (user): the FIRST pass ties into existing base domains only — no new effect classes wholesale.** Rock-domain tagging (the Tier 1 table below) is **REINSTATED as pass 1** — pure XML, and every tagged rock instantly gains the full combinatorial surface (basics solo, trigger/action combos with every base-game ingredient). The mutation-domain slate below stays the ambition for a **later pass**. New lever from the meal-generation dig, Claude recommendation awaiting ruling: **map T1 elemental rocks to the `*Minor` domains (basics only) and the deep four to the full domains (unlock "whenever X, Y" combos)** — depth tiering expressed entirely in existing XML keywords.

**Full pass-1 assignment DELIVERED 2026-07-22: `design/rock-eaten-effects.md` § "Cooking-domain assignment"** — 37 rocks tagged (every keyword verified live, precedent ingredient named per row), explicit no-domain and structurally-excluded lists, 6 flags awaiting rulings: the twins' pot-gamble (phosphorescent=`tastyMinor` vs radioactive=`selfPoison`, same name/sprite), the electric-has-no-Minor wart, three redundant pairs, the eaten/cooked unification option (defect's eat channel defaults to a random unit from the rock's cooking domain — one rule, tables can't drift), the grenade-rocks-detonate-in-the-pot verify, and the still-open PreservableItem grind fork.
User: "instead of making them for the rocks, i want them to be for the mutations themselves potentially." The cooking domains should echo the mod's six mutations (grants, triggers, and actions built from their machinery). Which items *carry* those domains (rocks, new ingredients, wall growths) is still open. The Tier 1 rock→base-domain table below is **superseded — kept as reference only**.

**Key enabler (decompiled 2026-07-22):** `ProceduralCookingEffectUnitMutation<T>` — generic base for mutation-grant units (Burgeoning/Cryokinesis/etc. all use it). Handles everything: grants the mutation at `AddedTier` (rolled, e.g. "1-2") if absent, **or +`BonusTier` levels (e.g. "2-3") if the eater already has it**, cleanup on expiry, real-mutation-gained edge case. A grant unit per mod mutation ≈ 10 lines. Meals double as mutation food for players actually running the mod's mutations — free synergy.

### Superseded proposal (reference) + still-live pieces
Three tiers, independently shippable, smallest first:

1. **Tier 1 — zero C#: existing domains on the rock catalog.** *(SUPERSEDED by the mutation-direction ruling — then REINSTATED as pass 1 by the 2026-07-22 existing-domains-first ruling; assignments below are the working draft again, pending the Minor/full tiering call.)* Draft assignment table:
   | rock | domain | base-game precedent |
   |---|---|---|
   | chunk of salt | `thirst` | Pickles |
   | fungal four (sporolith/mycolite/blightchert/capstone) | `fungus` | Pickled Mushrooms |
   | pyroclase | `heat` | Fire Ant Gaster Paste |
   | icy (rimestone/frostspar) | `cold` | Freeze-Dried Hoarshrooms |
   | galvena | `electric` | Spark Tick Plasma |
   | phasalt | `phase` | Phase Silk |
   | farflint | `teleport` | Fermented Yondercane |
   | grimslate | `fear` | Dried Lah Petals |
   | vantabsidian | `darkness` | Dried Vanta Petals |
   | lovestone | `love` | (Beetlebum honey et al.) |
   | soupstone | `tastyMinor` | Salthopper Chip |
   Notes: fungal rocks' cooking domain is static `fungus` regardless of the lottery (the domain is infection-neutral, same as the names — no conflict). Charges=1 each, stack-friendly.
   - **Design fork to rule — how rocks become ingredients:** (a) **tag the throwable rocks directly** — hoard-or-hurl grows a third verb, *or simmer*; ammo and pantry compete for the same stones; fragility doesn't interact (cooking consumes outright, no HoldChance roll); or (b) **grind step via `PreservableItem`** — preserve a rock at a campfire into N "ground {rock}" powder ingredients; keeps weapon/food identities separate, adds a deliberate conversion step, and **plain fallen rock (the anti-hoarding decay product) can get a grind product too, giving dull rocks a use**. (b) costs a small family of powder blueprints, still zero C#. *Claude leans (a) for elegance + (b)'s dull-rock grind as a single bonus entry; rule freely.*
2. **Tier 2 — small C#: a bespoke `stone` cooking domain** (`ProceduralCookingIngredient_stone` mapping + ~3 classes), carried by rocks that don't map cleanly above (plain rock, pebbles, rubble, shivshale, urchinite, stalactite…). Concept slate (unruled, mix-and-match):
   - *Unit (passive):* "Your skin sets like mortar: +2 AV, −3 DV" — distinct from armor domain's clean +2 AV by wearing the trade on its face.
   - *Trigger:* "whenever @thisCreature descends a level," (theme echo: Deeper strata shed stronger stone) — or reuse armor's suffered-penetration shape.
   - *Triggered action — the signature:* **"the ceiling above your enemies sheds stone"** — reuse Fissure's `DropRock` at 2–4 visible hostile cells, band 1, no spill. One meal ties the whole mod together; the machinery already exists and is battle-tested.
   - `RandomWeight` 10 so stone joins random-ingredient meals (base-game convention).
3. **Tier 3 — stretch: signature named recipe** ("stone soup" — soupstone + salt; the folk tale is exactly Qud's register). Specific recipes are `CookingRecipe` C# classes, learnable via water ritual (`WaterRitualCookingRecipe` conversation part exists) — park unless wanted.

**Wall growths (the original earmark):** harvestable mineral growths (wall-mounted, `Harvestable` part, Cooking and Gathering skill) that join the fall table and yield Tier 1/2 ingredients — content-shaped, natural fit for the creatures workstream when it opens; no engine risk (Harvestable is bog-standard).

### Mutation-domain slate (Claude, 2026-07-22 — ranked by strength as cooking effects; awaiting rulings)
Each domain gets up to three slots: passive **Unit**, **Trigger**, triggered **Action**. Grant-units are near-free (generic base above); triggers/actions reuse mod machinery. Claude's ranking, strongest first:

1. **Fissure — the signature.** Action: "the ceiling collapses around @thisCreature" — 3–5 `DropRock` falls at visible enemies (or a radius-2 self-ring), band 1, no spill; battle-tested code, dramatic table talk, and the falls yield rocks = loot from a meal. Trigger candidate (theme-perfect): "whenever @thisCreature descends a level," (pairs with "Deeper strata shed stronger stone"). Grant-unit: Fissure at level 1-2 / +2-3.
2. **Calcify — the panic bunker.** Action: "stone walls erupt from the ground around @thisCreature" — no base-game cooking effect makes walls; completely novel proc. Permanent walls from a meal proc = world-littering; acceptable (they're carvable, and Stoneshaper synergy is a feature). Trigger candidate: on suffering a 2X+ penetration (armor domain's shape).
3. **Gorgon's Gaze — the flesh-to-stone trade.** Unit or triggered effect: "@thisCreature's flesh sets like stone" — Effect_PartiallyPetrified's stat block worn as a *buff* (+2 AV, −20 MS, −10 QN, the trade on its face; effect class already exists, needs a no-initiator costume). Grant-unit of the gaze itself at level 1 is legal and spicy (a channel mutation as day-grant) — flag: save-or-die-adjacent in NPC hands stays player-only since meals are player-eaten.
4. **Stoneshaper — the stone companion.** Grant-unit is the clean play (carve your own muse that day; +levels for natives). Action alternative: "a stone ape rises to fight beside @thisCreature" — reuse the ape-statue DropRock brain-branch as a direct summon, 50–100 rounds (needs a Temporary rider — ape machinery exists, timer is new).
5. **Earthen Barrage — the gravel shield.** Action: "a gravel whorl encircles @thisCreature for N rounds" — Effect_GravelWhorl at fixed strength (say 30% deflect, +1 AV), decoupled from charge count (small ctor variant). Grant-unit works via the base (toggle abilities ride the normal mutate path) but a whole ammo-kit as a meal grant is clunky — whorl-only is the better face.
6. **Geomancy — the weakest fit.** Unit: "stones @thisCreature throws reveal the surrounding terrain" (relay part already listens; a property gate could extend it to any thrown stone) — pure utility, only sings for throwing builds; secrets domain already owns "reveal" space. Fine as a bonus unit inside another domain, weak as its own.

**Packaging recommendation (unruled):** not six domains — bundle like the base game does (heat domain = 4 units + 2 triggers + 4 actions). Suggest **2–3 domains**, e.g. *deep* (Fissure + Geomancy bits), *stonework* (Calcify + Stoneshaper), *gorgon* (gaze + stone-flesh); or one fat *terra* domain if ingredients will be few. Domain count should follow the ingredient plan — one domain per ingredient family.

- [ ] `[design]` Rule the ranking/slate: which mutations make the cut, which slot shapes (unit/trigger/action) per mutation.
- [ ] `[design]` Packaging: how many domains, and what carries them (rocks? new ingredients? wall growths? — the rock table above is dead as *domain mapping* but rocks can still be the *carriers* of mutation domains).
- [ ] `[design]` Numbers pass once slots are ruled (AddedTier/BonusTier rolls, proc magnitudes, durations).
