  - **Shape RULED: seek + bounce, NO return, single use** (user inverted the disc's economy — and the return-only option — deliberately: "seek and bounce, no return. single use disk?"). Thrown at (or near) a hostile, the stone flies the geomagnetic disc's flight: pathfinds as a flyer (walls block; `PathAsIfFlying` semantics), strikes the aimed target, chains to more hostiles in range, and **spends itself at the end of every completed flight** (shatter message + `sfx_destroy_stone`; no corpse). Single-use IS the cost — the cost fork closed itself.
  - **To-hit RULED: hybrid** — the aimed target auto-hits (never-miss soul preserved); bounce targets roll the disc's own d20+Agility vs DV and can flinch out of the way.
  - **Bounces RULED: 2 flat** (vs the disc's 5 on a tier-6 artifact). Per-leg range = base throw range + 6 (vs the disc's +12) — **RATIFIED 2026-07-24 ("streakschorl leg range fine").**
  - **Fallback:** no hostile reachable / aimed target beyond flight range / no flyable line → the handler declines and the **normal never-miss throw proceeds unchanged** (rock lands intact, step-fragile as before — utility throws at doors etc. still work).
  - **Build: new `Scripts/Part_StreakFlight.cs`** (`Cleo_TerraFirma_StreakFlightPart`, Bounces/Range params on the blueprint) — transcribed from decompiled `GeomagneticDisc` (BeforeThrown hijack + private DoThrow re-implemented); deliberately dropped: return leg, power plant (IPoweredPart/charge/power-load), two-discs-collision check, examine-failure flight, flex-phase provider (rocks take no mods). Kept: auto-seek when thrown at nothing (flood-search for a hostile), `ProjectileMovingEvent` dispatch per step (Gravel Whorl and friends still get their say), damage via the rock's own ThrownWeapon part through `GetThrownWeaponPerformanceEvent` (1d6 vibro/Vorpal rides every hit), `WeaponPseudoThrowHit`, both render paths (legacy console + particle VFX). Stack handling: `SplitFromStack()` flies exactly one. Sounds: the disc's own throw/impact sfx as defaults (a supersonic whoosh — retag if it grates). NPC throwers work free (handler is actor-agnostic).
  - **Transcription semantics accepted-for-now, flagged:** a ProjectileMovingEvent handler that *displaces* the stone mid-flight ends the flight with the stone restored to the thrower's context (the disc's own early-return behavior — "it found its way back"); non-displacing interceptions strike (HitOverride, counts as a bounce-grade hit) or just end the flight, then the stone is spent as normal. Also: the old accuracy-lend never-miss machinery now only runs on fallback throws — the known stale-lend note (TODO line ~295) may fire if a hijacked flight follows a lend; "cleaned on next use" still covers it.

Remaining: throw at a visible hostile = flight + aimed auto-hit + up to 2 bounce hits/flinches + the stone shatters at the end (message + stone sound, nothing left); throw at empty ground near hostiles = it seeks one; throw with NO hostiles around = normal never-miss throw, rock lands intact and step-breaks as before; stack of 3 = one flies, two stay; vibro penetration lands on flight hits (vorpal vs Cragmensch); walls actually block flight legs (no through-wall bounces); Gravel Whorl vs an incoming streakschorl (deflection = flight ends, note where the stone ends up); NPC thrower flies it; particle-VFX and classic-render modes both animate; save/load unaffected (part is stateless).

## Quarriers in the wild (side-content workstream 3 — ACTIVE 2026-07-22)

**IN SCOPE FOR 1.0 — RULED 2026-07-26 (the release-scope conversation): "unfortunately i'd
like to fit quarriers in the 1.0 release, it's just going to be tricky to implement, i
think."** Quarriers is a release pillar, not a post-1.0 headline; sub-scope within the
workstream (which pieces can trail to 1.1) still open. **Art bar RULED same exchange:
"i'm fine reusing tiles and items for the quarriers. some things will need their own
sprites, but i'm gradually ticking away at those"** — reuse is the 1.0 default, bespoke
sprites land as the user produces them (see design/asset-debt-tracker.md).

**Painter scope RULED 2026-07-26 (same conversation): "ideally we could have the canyon
painter fully realized in 1.0 with additional items and scenes for flavor added in later
on."** The painter SYSTEM ships complete in 1.0 — carve + drape, camps, lumps, canyon
wiring, the recipe primitive working end-to-end; the CONTENT LIBRARY (scene recipes,
dressing items, tableaus) is open-ended by design and grows post-release.

**1.0-core / trail docket (drafted at the same conversation — core list is CLAUDE
PROPOSAL endorsed in direction, two scope calls still OPEN):**
- **CORE 1.0:** packs in canyons with working mutation AI (the four-handler sitting) ·
  camps via the painter, fully realized · 3x3 lumps · faction entry + neutral reception +
  water ritual on the overseer · provisioner trade.
- **TRAIL (post-1.0 flavor, per the painter ruling):** cairn/musing tableaus · geode
  lottery · dromad ride-alongs · additional scene recipes and dressing items.
- **Both scope calls RESOLVED 2026-07-26 (same conversation):** **Mole-beast — IN 1.0,
  blocked on the user's creature concept, not on scope:** "the mole beast's design
  *settles* the weirdness question right. i have to come up with a creature for it and i
  just haven't yet. it's annoying but it'll come with time." No depth fork exists —
  whatever creature the user invents IS the design; nothing to build until it lands.
  **Stilt merchant — IN 1.0:** "easy inclusion i think - it's just provisioner with a
  tent attached" — implementation shape ruled in the same breath: reuse the provisioner
  blueprint + a StiltTents table entry (the SixDayTents recipe already recon'd below).
- **Writing sittings sheet (user OK'd 2026-07-26): `design/writing-sittings-1.0.md`** —
  every user-owed text debt under the sign-off policy, consolidated for burn-down.

Two committed concepts (user, ruled): **(1)** quarrier packs in the canyons carrying 3x3 lumps of rock they've carved free from the walls; **(2)** a Stilt bazaar merchant selling non-expiring fissure stones as a random tent spawn — cheap, one-use utility grenades to stock up on.

**Lore direction (user, ruled):** the stonecarvers came from outside Qud (why they're a mod, not basegame). No established holds — at most a small temporary camp they stop at on pilgrimages through Qud, drawn by its rich canyons and deep caves full of varied stone. They bring their culture, their mutations (the mod's seven), and a new lens on the game. Consequence: no procedural-village surface; everything is zone-build-time content that reaches existing saves in unvisited zones.

### Rulings
- [x] `[design]` **NPCs use their mutations — RULED 2026-07-22.** Any mutation a stonecarver type carries, its AI must actually cast — "gotta be able to fight back" against a mutant who opens fire on them. Scope consequence: whichever mutations land on the roster commit their AI work (Fissure: done; EB: advertisement handler + NPC toggle handling; Stoneshaper: NPC branch for the player-only cell-picking path + wall-and-muse targeting, the most complex). These handlers are shared infrastructure — the parked "wild creatures with innate mutations" design-budget item inherits them for free.
- [x] `[design]` **Reception — RULED 2026-07-22: neutral at baseline, trending positive.** At the start you're just another wandering adventurer — they don't start combat; at minimum they can trade with you. Implies a proper faction entry (neutral standing needs a home faction; killing quarriers for their lumps costs rep — the temptation loop is intended).
- [x] `[design]` **Water ritual — RULED 2026-07-22: yes.** Engine hook verified: `GivesRep` part on the legendary blueprint (Snapjaw Hero 0 precedent) puts the faction on the reputation screen, makes kills cost rep, and makes the legendary water-ritual-capable.
- [x] `[design]` **Pack structure — RULED 2026-07-22: legendary-pack shape.** Several distinct stonecarver types divvying up the work (variety is the point), one legendary leading them — exactly the base game's wild-legendary idiom. Verified: `SnapjawParty1` = pickeach roles at varied chances + `Snapjaw Hero 0` at Chance 3; a `-with-Warlord` guaranteed-leader variant table exists as precedent; hero blueprint = base-role inherit + hero `builder` (name/stat boosts) + `GivesRep` + `Role=Hero` tag.

### Open decisions (one at a time)
- [ ] `[design]` **ON THE TABLE (user's question): how do they get the quarried stone *out* of Qud?** Canon recon 2026-07-22 (Books.xml, *Across Moghra'yi* Vols. I–III): the only established overland route in/out of Qud is crossing the Moghra'yi, the Great Salt Desert; the canonical crossing traffic is **dromad merchant caravans** ("accounts given by fungi who crossed Moghra'yi on the backs of dromad merchants"; "O, how the dromads know!"). Named far lands beyond the desert: **the Sunderlies** (Vol. I — "towering sundered bluffs… canyon walls dotted with innumerable cave-mouths… colossal, earthly honeycombs," cave-clan freeholds, Clan Sotur descends from "Sotur Stonehands," sigil = a sundered mountain), **Athenreach** (Vol. II), **Oth, the Free City** (Vol. III). Mechanically nothing ever leaves the map — the answer only needs to manifest in what the player sees (direction of travel, pack-animal entries in the party table, camp contents, conversation text).
  - **User's current thinking (2026-07-22, slept on it, NOT yet ruled):** (1) stonecutters travel with dromad merchants sometimes; (2) they have their own beast of burden for transporting stone — maybe a vehicle with an interior; (3) maybe the beast is a huge mole-like thing that uses its claws to help dig out quarriable stone when it's not carrying it.
  - [x] `[design]` **Mole-beast RULED 2026-07-22: committed.** The stonecarvers' beast of burden is the huge mole-like digger. **No interior** (cut — cargo, not walk-in). **Burrowing via BurrowingClaws: in, but provisional** — may be cut later if it misbehaves (PathAsBurrower is the dial). OPEN: what makes the mole *Qud* — user wants it weirder than a merely-big animal; weirdness axis unruled.
  - **Mole-beast feasibility (2026-07-22, XML ground truth):** no mole-anything in basegame. The only civilized burrowers are the four mopango blueprints (a friendly *pilgrim* people — canon resonance noted) — all carry `BurrowingClaws`, and the AI dial is on the mutation tag itself: `PathAsBurrower="true"` = genuinely tunnels (pathfinder treats walls as diggable), `"false"` = claws without map-eating pathing (mopango use both deliberately). "Huge" = tile art + description + stats; all creatures are one cell, no multi-tile tech. "Digs out quarriable stone" has two altitudes: flavor-only (pure XML) vs mechanically real (small C# hooking its wall destruction to yield rock items — mod already owns wall↔rock machinery). `Interior` composes orthogonally with claws/brain.
  - **Feasibility recon (2026-07-22, XML ground truth):** *Dromad ride-alongs* — wilderness tables seed `DromadTrader` groups at Chance 2; trader blueprint carries a `DromadCaravan` part that conjures the entourage. Shape (a): dromad-trader entry in our own pack table — pure XML, nothing to verify. Shape (b): merge stonecarvers into the caravan entourage — depends on whether `DromadCaravan` is table-driven or hardcoded (one-function decompile check at implementation). *Interior beast* — `Interior` part is XML-attachable to any creature/object (`Cell` → merged `<cell>` in Worlds.xml `Interior` world → `.rpm` map, same tech as camp chunks; mecha cockpit 5x3, Mover atrium 16x14). `Vehicle` part (pilots, power, EMP) is separate and optional — a living beast needs only `Interior`. Caveats: no basegame precedent for an *autonomous wandering* creature with an interior (mechas move piloted, other interiors are stationary) — decompile + smoke-test "interior follows wanderer" and "beast dies: what happens to interior contents" before spec. Design note: stone stacked inside the beast = the temptation loop, one layer deeper.
  - **Beast REVISED 2026-08-07 (carve-jam session, user direction): the mole becomes a giant STONE-EATING CENTIPEDE.** Supersedes the mole body plan in the 2026-07-22 ruling (the beast-of-burden commitment itself stands; BurrowingClaws-for-pathing dial carries over). User's fiction (user owns concept): vast — "potentially spanning a mile" — a walking wagon for the caravan, outfitted with months-if-not-years of supplies from home; quarried stone spread evenly along its back so as not to crush it, doubling as protective-but-breakable armor; mouth = darner dragonfly nymph labium × excavator; legs instead of hands. Stone-eating ties it to the Lithovore biology — dig-is-eat, one organ, one gesture (Claude framing, user-aligned). Carve-signature dials (DRAFT, Claude numbers-territory): wide low bore, sinuous low-frequency weave, walls smoothed with fine parallel leg-striations, ZERO spoil + polished-pebble gastrolith dung as trail (the dung rhymes with the feeding-sty forage note in the room jam).
    - [x] `[design]` **Single tile RULED 2026-08-07 (user: "that's what qud would do")** — star-kraken idiom: vast in fiction, one tile in play, the mile lives in prose. **Claude's head-tile + scenery-segment-chain proposal CUT by this ruling** — no multi-object body, ever. Consequences: cargo-armor = breakable equipped armor and/or inventory death-drops on the one creature (no statue-spill machinery needed); the caravan-robbing temptation stays on pack lumps + the beast's own inventory/armor.
    - [ ] `[design]` **Mouth/weapon OPEN, two user candidates:** (a) face-slot labium-excavator natural weapon (Horns-pattern head-mounted natural + BurrowingClaws dig tag; optional Sticky-Tongue-style pull for the labium shot) — **Claude lean: the dig=eat unification argument**; (b) frontal claw naturals. User's words: "could also see the case for frontal claw natural weapons."
    - [ ] `[design]` **Name OPEN** (user: "not really?") — candidate-slate session on request, naming-session idiom. Direction space floated (Claude): drover's working word / geological word / untranslated home-word (composes with the homeland-mystery dial).
  - **Mole-FOLK species axis (user float 2026-08-07, NOT yet ratified):** the stonecarvers themselves partly mole *people* — Barathrumite-idiom mixed faction, "though not uniformly." Mole variants carry BurrowingClaws naturals; human variants keep pickaxes (camps still littered with them). Professions remain the face — species is a second axis under the ruled six-role roster (snapjaw variant-blueprint idiom, cheap). **RESHAPED same session (user: "I think we could live with one sprite for each stonecarver") — species is FIXED PER PROFESSION, not rolled per individual:** each of the six roles commits to one species, one sprite. Consequences: sprite debt stays at six (user's to draw); look-descriptions single-body (text debt unchanged); pack tableau keeps the six-silhouette legibility the profession-first ruling was built for; the mixed faction displays in every pack by construction (a rolled axis could chance into all-human). NEW OPEN (user's, concepts theirs): **which roles are mole-folk** — considerations floated (Claude, unruled): claws pull toward digging trades (hewer; drover has burrower-tends-burrower logic); provisioner-as-human has an outsider-facing-face argument; overseer species = loudest choice (water-ritual face); mason species reads as theology. Claude proposals riding on it, unruled: **hands are cultural, not anatomical** — claw or pick, a mason cuts square (preserves the two-voice carve legibility; the claws' showcase = the soft round spaces: divots, satellites); PathAsBurrower=false on camp residents so claws don't eat the map.
  - **Mopango symmetry (lore note, user insight 2026-08-07 — mopango are PANGOLINS, no mole collision):** mopango = cave-faring pilgrim folk with Light Manipulation who naturally light up wherever they go; the stonecarvers are cave-faring pilgrims whose passage leaves *shape* — carving, sockets, cairns; the beast's trail (bore-mouth + pebble dung) joins the same ladder. Two cultures marking the dark, each in its own medium. The escalation ladder (cairn → musing spot → camp) is the mechanism that delivers this read.

  - **Profession-first structure (Claude lean, 2026-07-22; user floated provisioner/mason as example roles):** distinguish types by profession noun — the verified basegame idiom for civilized packs (Goatfolk Hornblower/Sower/Yurtwarden/Shaman vs snapjaws' combat nouns; Yurtwarden = identity-is-tending-the-camp precedent). Three layers: profession (face — name/gear/description/camp presence), mutation (engine each profession works with), `Role` tag (AI layer: Minion/Brute/Artillery/Leader). Why: profession identity holds even where combat AI isn't built yet (mason reads as mason without casting); "provisioner" maps directly onto `GenericInventoryRestocker` (dromad-trader machinery — makes the pack itself the trading stop, cashing in the reception ruling's "at least they can trade"); work-division makes the pack tableau legible at a glance. Roster ruling = name the professions + which mutation each carries.
  - **Per-type cost map (recon):** *Fissure* — free, NPC-complete since the rebuild (AI handler in). *EB* — **RULED 2026-07-22: real EB mutation on the thrower type** (Boulderer-mimic fork closed); cost = AI advertisement handler + NPC toggle handling (moderate). *Stoneshaper* — ~~free if identity-only~~ **identity-only option closed by the NPCs-use-their-mutations ruling**; **cost re-estimated DOWN after full code read (2026-07-22): ~40-60 lines, nothing novel.** The complexity was all player-UI (two-stage PickDestinationCell dialogue + goto retry loops); NPC side collapses: muse := `E.Target` (AI commands carry the combat target; statue copies attacker's Str/Agi/Tou — self-balancing), wall := nearest-valid-cell scan reusing the existing validation gauntlet factored into a predicate (~15 lines; canyon habitat is wall-rich by definition), AI handler advertises only when a valid wall is in radius. Heavy machinery (MakeStatueOfCreatureFromWall/ApplyStatueBuffs/DeanimateStatue) already static + actor-agnostic; allegiance via generic `BecomeCompanionOf`; swap prompt + Lovesick popup already IsPlayer-gated. Needs a small refactor extracting the inline commit path (creation→cooldown bookkeeping) for both branches to share. Side-findings: player wall-pick is ~~unbounded~~ **sight-gated (verified 2026-07-22 vs decompiled `IComponent.PickDestinationCell`: `Range = 9999` but `VisLevel = AllowVis.OnlyVisible` by default, mod doesn't override)** — standard "range: sight" semantics, nothing to fix; Lv10+ NPC would auto-swap statues (prompt is player-only) — moot if pack stonecarvers stay below Lv10 (roster-spec number). *Random* — `RandomMutations` part precedented (esper hero rolls 4-5 mentals); restricting the pool to our seven needs a decompile check, else bespoke ~20-line part; **randomness doesn't dodge AI-readiness** — non-ready rolls yield NPCs that never cast. *Structure note:* types-by-gear/stats IS the snapjaw idiom (shared base + variant blueprints); the two ideas compose.

### Lore session 2026-07-24 (chat leanings — nothing ruled unless marked)
- **Stonecarvers are MUTANTS, not True Kin — user, firm. NO CALLING WRINKLE (corrected 2026-07-24):** callings ARE the mutant chargen category (`Mutated Human Subtypes="Callings"` / `True Kin Subtypes="Castes"`, Genotypes.xml) — the shipped Stonecarver calling was mutant-side all along. The "True Kin chargen" claim was an error in the old merge note (line ~35, now fixed) that Claude propagated despite a standing memory saying the opposite. Nothing to reconcile; the floated apprentice/rename options are void.
- **Homeland: no hard canon assignment — user ("touchy," not strictly opposed).** Claude pitch, awaiting ruling: the carvers never name their home (diaspora identity — ask a carver where home is and they tap the nearest wall). Dial open: full mystery vs speculation-allowed (unreliable in-world scholars may guess — Sunderlies among the guesses, framed as hearsay per the *Across Moghra'yi* idiom) vs invented homeland.
- **Creed draft (pitched, user "mostly fine"):** stone remembers; carving is waking, not making; Qud's stone is *awake* (the pilgrimage reason); the mutations are the stone answering. Spent-stone rites unify `DullAfter` + the "You return the stone to the earth" reabsorb text as one belief.
- **Geode lottery — user likes (had considered it independently).** Sealed geodes as mystery grab-bags in Stilt-merchant stock; crack → population table roll (mundane rock / brine / gemstone / rare **viewing stone** jackpot).
- **Camp — leaning commit, built manually.** User: base procgen too flimsy to hook; consistent with the ruled "no procedural-village surface." Tech: static chunk skeleton + population tables + placement hints (SixDayTents idiom) — fixed bones, rolled furnishings. Strange-mood tableau (one mason stood "listening" at a raw block, camp working around them) floated as the set-piece.
  - [x] `[design]` **Painter architecture RULED 2026-07-24 (user: "genius — solves a problem I've had since their inception"):** recursive recipe primitive. A recipe = footprint requirement + fixed placements + weighted blanks; a blank resolves to an object, a table roll, or another recipe (nesting dolls). Hard depth cap 3 (camp → room → cluster). Fit rule: try smaller variant, else skip — NO backtracking/solver/adjacency logic ever (that's the flimsy procgen being avoided). Carve-vs-drape is a per-recipe attribute, not two systems. Every recipe logs placed/shrunk/dropped in the TF-VERIFY idiom. Payoff: recipes reuse across contexts (grave = lone corridor cairn AND camp blank — recombination reads as unauthored narrative); strange-mood mason = rare roll in one workshop blank, not a special skeleton. Two independent dials: spawn weight (camp rarity across canyons) vs recipe tables (variety within a camp). Middle ground achieved: authored texture at procedural density — neither one overdesigned static zone nor canyon-spam. Same primitive later serves the Unfinished One, lithovore restaurant, Stilt dressing.
  - [x] `[design]` **Text sign-off policy RULED 2026-07-24 (user):** all AI-produced text is DRAFT until the user rewrites or signs off — text debt clears at sign-off, not at generation, and weighs the same as sprite debt. Context: Claude asserted a wrong basic fact (calling=True Kin) the same session it pitched writing an anchor book; the user's standing all-text-is-draft rule exists for exactly this. Anchor book stays unwritten unless the user asks for a raw-material draft or writes it themselves.
  - [x] `[design]` **NPC conversation scope RULED 2026-07-24: no bespoke dialogue trees.** All pack NPCs use the base-game minor-NPC idiom — one flavorful greeting line + the standard trade option ("Live and drink" precedent). User: "same amount of work, presented differently" — the writing budget goes into six great greeting lines + look-descriptions, which reads MORE in-universe than scripted trees. Trade = engine (restocker table on provisioner); water ritual = engine (GivesRep on overseer). Doctrine rulings (consent etc.) now flavor greetings/descs rather than gating a conversation layer.

(2) feeding sty — hay+worms, carved runnel from wall seep; +mineral lick, +gastrolith dung (polished-pebble forage, rare good stone) [Claude]. (5) block yard — graded rows (raw→blocked→dressed→marked), placed DEEP (walk through their life to rob them — temptation dial); +spent-stone midden adjacent [Claude]. New rooms [Claude]: (6) sleeping niches — 1-cell honeycomb cells in the pocket wall w/ personal-effects markers (comb resonance, no homeland named); (7) shrine niche — uncarvable stone on plinth, boundary eyes, offerings (most stealable object; sacrilege hook); (8) mill-pantry — provisioner's station. Grounding already shipped: the calling's gear table includes a Vinewood Sap Mask for exactly this reason (PopulationTables.xml) — reuse the detail in descriptions/conversation [Claude]. Rooms = sub-recipes included by pocket size (small=communal+sty; large=all).

Scale by adding nodes, never by inflating rooms — every chamber stays den-sized (coziness preserved structurally; first slice at 4/3 radii read as a hall, confirmed the failure mode). Comb-cave resonance via pure architecture, no homeland named. Painter primitive set: **blob** (node), **satellite** (1-cell alcove, needs back-wall puncture guard), **spine** (1-wide tunnel, straight or one L-bend, NO pathfinding — hand-dug reads as the obvious line). Divot tunnel = spine + satellites, composition not new code. Sleeping: small camp = 2–3 perimeter alcoves (tfpaint v3, next build, zero assets — bedroll + Random Copper Figurine effects roll echoes the calling's own maquette); large camp = divot hall/tunnels, size-gated by recipe table.

Phase 1 carves greedily (primitives accumulate where wall supports them, "looking right" is the only criterion — iterate bare geometry via tf-wishes with zero dressing noise); output is an inventory of spaces with metadata (shape class, size, dead-end?, mouth-distance). Phase 2 dressers claim spaces by priority — required roles claim best-fit qualifying spaces, skip-if-none (no backtracking, unchanged creed); leftovers get flavor rolls or nothing. Origin: bearsharktopus procgen piece jam (bsky, this session) — "quarried tunnels and niches that people could transform into living areas"; generator now mirrors the fiction (carve first, inhabit after). Engine precedent confirmed same day: CaveCity IS this pipeline (see `design/cavegen-survey-2026-08-07.md`).

Camp roll 30% (lane starts beyond the gallery span, offsets 10/12/15 — first anchor sat inside the gallery's own open span, always failed) + standalone **Ranks** button. Verified: 3/24 camps place ranks, standalone works, 0 sealed. Unit cores join wrap centers as phase-2 feature slots.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 6, 2026-08-08 (user's seventh drawing): SPARSENESS — the comb vocabulary DISAGGREGATED.** The drawing is scattered fragments at hermit scale: a lone ring, a small knot, spurs, loosely linked with real distance between. Implemented as the sprawl table's rare jackpot (the Red Rock Weight-1-shrine idiom): **lone cell** — a single ring unit alone in the wall (carveRanks forced to 1 unit), 8% of sprawl rolls, front-adjacency connects it. Sprawl table rebalanced: nibble 32 / slot 14 / notch 27 / stepped 11 / island 8 / cell 8. Verified 30 zones: 5 lone cells (~1 in 6 zones), 0 sealed. The register spectrum is now complete: sprawl bites (accumulated work) → lone cells (hermit) → comb (organic warren) → ranks (planned settlement) — one vocabulary, four social densities.
    - **WEAVE + GUARANTEE + MOTIF MIX, 2026-08-08 (user: "weave these into each other and secure that something other than the main living core spawns... break up the blueprints into parts to mix and match"):** (a) **guarantee ladder** — secondary rolls (sleep 45 / comb 60 / ranks 30) now fall back to a forced comb→ranks→sleep attempt when all miss: every full camp gets ≥1 structure beyond the living core (22/28; the rest are honest thin-band core-onlys or waystops); at exactly one structure, 50% reaches for a SECOND. (b) **territorial fix** — the placers were starving each other (identical seam offsets; 0 multi-structure camps in 28): comb spots extended deeper both flanks, ranks tries both flanks → **7/28 multi-structure camps** incl. double-combs. (c) **weave pass** — structures link to their neighbors by extra spines (≤26 apart, 75%, max 2), the star becomes a cyclic network (18/28 camps); spines now REFUSE kept walls (posts/islands/rank-cores registered) so a blocked weave dead-ends honestly instead of gutting a room. (d) **motif mix** — carveComb's parts (rooms/wraps/teeth/stubs) are no longer all-rolled-always: each comb draws 2-4 parts from a weighted menu and logs them (`[rooms+wraps]`, `[stubs+teeth]`...) — the user's blueprint-parts framing made structural; districts within one camp differ in character. Verified 30 zones: 0 sealed throughout.
    - **SEALED-DONUT BUGFIX, 2026-08-08 (user reroll verdict #1: "lone disconnected 3x3/3x4 rooms with a pillar in the middle"):** the wrap move never carved a connector back to the walk — rings floated sealed unless the walk happened to brush them. Fix: wraps must find a DOOR CANDIDATE (ring cell orthogonally adjacent to walk-visited lattice, outside the skip arc) BEFORE carving — no candidate, no wrap — and the connector carves after the ring. Wrap rate 11→4 per ~20 combs (strict but every one reachable). **Plus a permanent ORPHAN TRIPWIRE:** the shell pass runs a fresh post-dress flood from the mouth and counts unreachable camp cells per camp (first version reused the pre-dress flood and false-flagged every alcove). Verified 30 zones: 28/28 camps at 0 orphans, 0 sealed. This class of bug can no longer ship silently.
    - **DEEP PASS + BACK-PASSAGES, 2026-08-08 (user reroll verdicts #2-3: "very few quarry sites are interconnected... huge swathes of plain stone where we could be doing a bit more"):** root cause — everything carved clung to the corridor's skin. (a) **Deep pass** (new genZone stage): 2-4 structures planted in genuinely deep mass (no open floor within 3) — pocket 35 / small comb 30 / deep gallery 20 / lone cell 15 — each tunneled to the nearest daylight (spiral search ≤22) and 80% chained to its neighbor: shafts into darkness, the DF-mine read. Deep combs may carve unattached — the shaft is their door. (b) **Back-passages**: sprawl records its placed workings (locality-sorted); consecutive pairs ≤12 apart link behind the face at 40% — quarry sites form districts. Verified 25 zones: 66 deep sites (~2.6/zone), 18 chains, 10 back-passages, 0 orphans, 0 sealed.
    - **THE DIGGER, 2026-08-08 (user: "is this a good case for the drillbot to actually work for construction? some generations that just had one making patterns"):** agent-based construction — one simulated digger whose TRACE is the generation. (Clarification banked: Qud's Drillbot is only a pathfinder movement profile; this is the stronger behavioral-agent idea, and in-game it's just a loop in the builder — no creature, no AI ticks.) Behavior: momentum-4/curl-2/anti-1 walk preferring fresh rock (35% hesitation re-walking own dig), action rolls — 3x3 lump-socket 6% (the quarrier rhyme), island room 3%, rest pocket 2%, branch-and-return 4% (stack ≤4), turn-around on stall (≤6), respects kept walls + protected shells. Budget 90-150 actions. **Connectivity guaranteed BY CONSTRUCTION — the orphan class cannot occur in a trace.** Standalone Digger button + 30% of zones get one. First stall bug (9-step runs: couldn't re-walk own tunnel, died in first corner) fixed same session. Traces run 76-114 steps, 1-4 sockets, 2-6 branches. Dials noted: room/pocket rates are conservative — tune on verdicts.
    - **SMALL-AND-WINDING RULING, 2026-08-08 (user: "deprioritize large rooms and just figure out how to hone our weird winding hallways and smallish rooms"):** (a) gallery fit-ladder flipped smallest-first, **9x7 grand gallery CUT** — 5x5 colonnade is now the default working, 7x5 the rare big one; (b) comb rooms bias 2x2-dominant while camp lattices LENGTHEN (5-7) — more hall per comb, less chamber; (c) **`windingTunnel` primitive** (noise-cost cardinal Dijkstra, kept-walls near-impassable, shells discouraged-but-doorable) replaces straight L-spines in ALL connective tissue — weave links, deep-site shafts, deep chains, sprawl back-passages. **The camp's main spine stays mason-straight deliberately: the two-voice contrast (chalk-line vs wander) is the ruled design.** Verified 20 zones: 0 orphans, 0 sealed, ~83ms/zone.
    - **FRESH-SLATE RESET, 2026-08-08 (user ruling: "the initial language that's baked in is kind of poisoning what we're trying now — generate it on a fresh blank slate, then try generating it with canyons, then generate it painted [= decorated, has items]"):** the legacy camp chain (mouth→home→spine→gallery→face, the session's oldest code) is recognized as an inherited skeleton every zone still wears under the patches. **Three-stage rebuild plan: (1) BLANK SLATE — the honed language composing alone, DELIVERED as `growWarren`/Warren button:** network growth with no chain — ports (frontier cells), winding hall from a port, small part rolled at its end (room 22 / mini-wrap 16 / ring unit 14 / mini comb 14 / pocket 14 / socket pair 10 / honest dead-end 10), 18% loop-backs between ports, 12-18 parts per warren. First rolls: 34-45 halls, 4-9 loops, full part spread — reads as the user's drawings 1-2 directly. **(2) IN CANYON — same generator constrained by real bands/mouths (next sitting, after stage-1 verdicts). (3) PAINTED — dressed with items (the deferred phase-2 tables).** The old Zone pipeline stays intact for comparison; it is now the LEGACY track. Verdict cycle: reroll Warren, judge the pure language, tune, then advance stages.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUNDS 7-8, 2026-08-08 (user's eighth + ninth drawings, "two more examples to refine with") — first rounds tuned against the FRESH warren:** drawing 8 gave the local grammar — **hooks** (G-spiral stubs curling around a wall pocket, 5% part roll) and **footed dead-ends** (stubs that turn once at the tip, replacing flat dead-ends). Drawing 9 gave a second HALL VOICE — **trunks** (30% of halls): long (12-24), mostly straight with sparse 15% single jogs — the staircase-step hand, purposeful travel vs the local wander; trunk convergence produces drawing 9's central-knot structure emergently. Verified: all features fire (5-16 trunks, 1-4 hooks, 0-3 feet, 0-11 loops per warren). Corpus now nine drawings; the warren speaks both the near hand and the far hand.
    - **NO-TUNNEL-WITHOUT-A-REASON, 2026-08-08 (user verdict: "warren still generates a lot of empty, long spaces rather than tunnels-into-rooms — but I might be diluting the thing that makes it feel like a stonecarver's quarry"):** the design answer, banked — **tunnels-into-rooms is dungeon logic; no-tunnel-without-a-reason is quarry logic.** A dungeon's halls deliver destinations; a quarry's halls exist because every foot cost labor — the threat to quarry-feel is purposelessness and REGULARITY of payoff, not rooms per se. Built: (a) **every hall ends in work** — failed part rolls fall back room→socket→prospector's-foot (an abandoned test-dig is also work); (b) **trunks are haulage-ways** — 12%/step side-bites along their length (loading bays), length trimmed 12-24→10-20; (c) local halls trimmed 4-10→4-8. Effect: halls-to-parts ratio collapsed ~3:1 → ~1:1 (12-17 halls now yield 15-17 parts vs 34-45 halls for the same parts before) — the bare-hall bloat is gone while payoff KIND stays irregular (chamber / sockets / feet). The quarry-ness lives in work-evidence + extraction causality + fit-rule irregularity, all preserved.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 9, 2026-08-08 (user's tenth drawing, "constrained rooms"): the SUITE** — a bounded footprint (7-10 × 5-7, strict all-wall validation) whose interior is subdivided into 2-4 small rooms by shared 1-cell partitions, doors punched per partition, ONE entrance from the arriving hall. The motif the warren lacked: rooms PACKED against each other (domestic architecture behind a door) vs the isolated dilations everywhere else. 14% part roll (part table rebalanced: room 16 / suite 14 / wrap 14 / unit 12 / comb 10 / pocket 12 / sockets 10 / hook 6 / foot 6); fires in ~1/3 of warrens (footprint is demanding — fits where the network left space). Dial noted: feet counts run high (7-10/warren — the work-guarantee ladder lands on prospector's-feet often in dense networks); tune if the user reads it as stubby. Corpus: ten drawings.
    - **PROTECTED SHELL added same session (user: "central areas spawn with a third of their walls gone"):** root cause — pocket walls validated at carve time but eaten afterward: sprawl bites tolerate 15-30% open footprint (can punch through a 1-cell pocket wall), and the pinch police would happily OPEN a diagonal pinch between pocket interior and corridor. Fix: genQuarry collects every camp-chain cell (home/threshold/spine/gallery/face/alcoves/comb/bore/digs/back-door), marks interiors `protectedFloor` + all adjacent walls `protectedShell` (8-dir, ~70-150 walls/camp); sprawl bites reject any footprint touching either; police never clears shell walls. Verified 30 zones: 29 camps, 0 sealed, pockets stay interior.
  - **CARVE ARTICULATION BATCH 2026-08-07 (user: "carved sections should feel fully articulated before filling them") — geometry-only, from the ruin recon (`cavegen-survey-2026-08-07.md` § freshness):** (1) **threshold squeeze** — home pocket always sits 1-3 cells behind the corridor, entry carved 1-wide: deliberate den-mouth geometry, and it fixes the **SEALED-CAMP BUG** (deeper home retries left wall between mouth and blob tip, camps opened only by weathering luck — latent in BOTH sim and C#; **C# fix built + deployed same day**, the one articulation item already ported). (2) **grammar police** (Ruiner's CleanNarrowConnections idiom): diagonal pinches opened, accidental 1-cell wall shards swept — island-notch remnants protected via keep-set; ~5 fixes/zone, runs after sprawl. (3) **back door** (30% of full camps, when a ≤9-cell wall run separates the wing from the corridor): 1-wide cut making the warren a loop — lived-in burrows have two exits. (4) **connectivity self-check**: flood-fill from the mouth asserts home + wing reachable, logged in the TF-VERIFY idiom. Verified 30 zones: 27 camps, **0 sealed**, 3 back doors, 28 pinches + 112 shards policed. Items 2-4 join the sim-ahead port-pending list.

Growth rolls after the face: 45% second sleeping node further along the seam (blob+spine+alcoves), 35% **beast bore** from the gallery into the massif (smoothed lining — reads spectacularly), 30% dead-end test dig off the spine. Verified 30 Qud-faithful zones: 19 full camps / 9 waystops / 2 spawn-nothing; all extras firing. **C# tfquarry still has only: bend + drift variants + single-mouth walk (deployed earlier today) — the restart/waystop/extras layer is sim-only until ported.**
  - **SIM FINDING #1, same day (the tool paid for itself on first click): the straight camp chain CANNOT fit a real canyon zone.** Mouth→home→spine→gallery→face runs ~20 cells deep in one line; an 80x25 zone with an E-W corridor leaves N/S wall bands only ~6-12 thick. 2/12 seeds fit. **Fix (both sim AND C#, deployed): the working wing turns PERPENDICULAR after the home pocket** — quarries drift along the seam, parallel to the corridor, not into the mountain — **and the placement walks the seam** (both lateral directions × spine lengths 8/6/10/12, first gallery fit wins, no backtracking-with-state, just candidate spots). Also multi-mouth retry for the home blob (sim tries 8 candidates).

After both fixes: 17/20 seeds produce a full camp (was 2/12); the 3 misses are honestly-thin canyons = the ruled spawn-nothing fallback working.

**Tier 1 proof-of-concept BUILT + DEPLOYED 2026-07-24 (compiles clean; UNTESTED — first live run
next boot).** `Scripts/_TestHarness.cs`: wish-driven in-game scenario runner that drives
PLAYER-path actions through the real engine pipeline and asserts on ENGINE STATE directly (the
answer to "log inference is not proof" — an in-code assertion of slot/inventory state IS the
direct evidence). Results land as `[TF-VERIFY] [AUTO] SCENARIO x: PASS/FAIL - detail` lines plus
in-game messages. Rides the `Helpers.VERIFY_WATCH` kill switch — **joins the strip-before-release
obligation (Cross-cutting entry updated).**
